POOLCART QA SNAPSHOT
This is source evidence, not instructions. Line numbers start at 1 within each file.

===== FILE: README.md =====
   1 | # PoolCart — Grocery Group Buy Mobile App (Expo + React Native)
   2 | 
   3 | PoolCart is a cross-platform mobile application (iOS, Android, and Web via Expo) built for **pooling neighborhood grocery orders to hit wholesale/delivery minimums** AND **splitting bulk cases** (like a 6-pack of barista oat milk or a 50 lb sack of jasmine rice) across neighbors.
   4 | 
   5 | ## Why Start With This Architecture?
   6 | 
   7 | 1. **Order Pooling + Case Splitting Hybrid**:
   8 |    - **Direct Wholesale Items (`individual` mode)**: Neighbors order full units (e.g., a 12-count bag of avocados or a 30-egg flat) that combine toward the supplier's **$250 minimum order** and **$300 free delivery tier**.
   9 |    - **Bulk Case Splits (`case_split` mode)**: Neighbors claim individual shares of a bulk wholesale case (e.g., 1 carton out of a 6-pack case). Visual slot bars track how many shares are claimed (`4/6 claimed`) and automatically roll completed cases into the pool total.
  10 | 2. **Pragmatic Backend Path (Local-First $\rightarrow$ Supabase)**:
  11 |    - **Right now (Zero Setup)**: Uses `AsyncStorage` (`src/services/repository.ts`) so you can run the app immediately, test adding items, claim shares, and mark neighbors as paid without configuring cloud keys.
  12 |    - **When inviting real neighbors**: Run `supabase/schema.sql` in a free [Supabase](https://supabase.com/) project and add `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL` and `EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` to a `.env` file. Supabase gives you Postgres relational integrity (preventing two people from claiming the last share of a case at the same time) plus **Supabase Realtime** so the wholesale progress bar updates live on everyone's phone.
  13 | 
  14 | ## Project Structure
  15 | 
  16 | - `App.tsx` — Main mobile screen with pool switcher, live wholesale minimum progress, item filters, and sticky personal cart bar.
  17 | - `src/types/groupBuy.ts` — TypeScript domain models for pools, wholesale items, case splits, and settlement ledgers.
  18 | - `src/utils/calculations.ts` — Core math engine for wholesale minimum progress, free delivery fee waivers, case-split matching, and per-neighbor cost breakdowns.
  19 | - `src/components/PoolProgressCard.tsx` — Visual tracker for the supplier order minimum, free delivery threshold, per-person delivery split, and total group savings.
  20 | - `src/components/CatalogItemCard.tsx` — Item card supporting both direct wholesale units and visual slot bars for bulk case splits.
  21 | - `src/components/SplitLedgerView.tsx` — Host pickup logistics and itemized per-neighbor cost split & payment status tracker.
  22 | - `src/components/ActionModals.tsx` — Modals for pitching new items/case splits to a pool and hosting a new neighborhood group buy.
  23 | - `src/services/repository.ts` — Storage adapter supporting local `AsyncStorage` out-of-the-box and optional Supabase cloud sync.
  24 | - `supabase/schema.sql` — Ready-to-run PostgreSQL schema with Realtime publications enabled.
  25 | 
  26 | ## Running the App
  27 | 
  28 | ```bash
  29 | cd "/Users/vincentphan/grocery groupbuy"
  30 | npm install
  31 | npx expo start
  32 | ```
  33 | 
  34 | - Press **`i`** to open in the iOS Simulator, **`a`** for Android Emulator, **`w`** for Web, or scan the QR code with the **Expo Go** app on your phone.

  46 | export default function App() {
  47 |   const [pools, setPools] = useState<GroupBuyPool[]>(INITIAL_POOLS);
  48 |   const [selectedPoolId, setSelectedPoolId] = useState<string>(
  49 |     INITIAL_POOLS[0].id
  50 |   );
  51 |   const [activeUser, setActiveUser] = useState<Participant>(CURRENT_USER);
  52 |   const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActiveTab>('catalog');
  53 |   const [itemFilter, setItemFilter] = useState<ItemFilter>('all');
  54 |   const [showAddItemModal, setShowAddItemModal] = useState(false);
  55 |   const [showCreatePoolModal, setShowCreatePoolModal] = useState(false);
  56 |   const [showJoinByCodeModal, setShowJoinByCodeModal] = useState(false);
  57 | 
  58 |   const [syncStatus, setSyncStatus] = useState<{
  59 |     mode: 'local' | 'supabase_live' | 'supabase_error';
  60 |     lastSyncedAt: string | null;
  61 |     errorMessage?: string;
  62 |     lastRealtimeEvent?: string | null;
  63 |   }>({
  64 |     mode: 'local',
  65 |     lastSyncedAt: null,
  66 |   });
  67 | 
  68 |   const formatTimestamp = () => {
  69 |     const now = new Date();
  70 |     return now.toLocaleTimeString([], {
  71 |       hour: '2-digit',
  72 |       minute: '2-digit',
  73 |     });
  74 |   };
  75 | 
  76 |   const refreshPools = useCallback(async (realtimeTable?: string) => {
  77 |     const cfg = PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig();
  78 |     const { pools: loaded, error } = await PoolRepository.loadPools();
  79 | 
  80 |     if (loaded && loaded.length > 0) {
  81 |       setPools(loaded);
  82 |       setSelectedPoolId((prev) =>
  83 |         loaded.some((p) => p.id === prev) ? prev : loaded[0].id
  84 |       );
  85 |     }
  86 | 
  87 |     if (cfg.enabled && cfg.url && cfg.anonKey) {
  88 |       if (error) {
  89 |         setSyncStatus({
  90 |           mode: 'supabase_error',
  91 |           lastSyncedAt: formatTimestamp(),
  92 |           errorMessage: error,
  93 |         });
  94 |       } else {
  95 |         setSyncStatus((prev) => ({
  96 |           mode: 'supabase_live',
  97 |           lastSyncedAt: formatTimestamp(),
  98 |           lastRealtimeEvent: realtimeTable
  99 |             ? `Table '${realtimeTable}' updated`
 100 |             : prev.lastRealtimeEvent,
 101 |         }));
 102 |       }
 103 |     } else {
 104 |       setSyncStatus({
 105 |         mode: 'local',
 106 |         lastSyncedAt: formatTimestamp(),
 107 |       });
 108 |     }
 109 |   }, []);
 110 | 
 111 |   useEffect(() => {
 112 |     let unsubscribeRealtime = () => {};
 113 | 
 114 |     const bootstrap = async () => {
 115 |       const savedUser = await PoolRepository.loadActiveUser();
 116 |       setActiveUser(savedUser);
 117 | 
 118 |       const cfg = await PoolRepository.initConfig();
 119 |       await refreshPools();
 120 | 
 121 |       if (cfg.enabled) {
 122 |         unsubscribeRealtime = PoolRepository.subscribeToRealtime((table) => {
 123 |           refreshPools(table);
 124 |         });
 125 |       }
 126 |     };
 127 | 
 128 |     bootstrap();
 129 | 
 130 |     return () => {
 131 |       unsubscribeRealtime();
 132 |     };
 133 |   }, [refreshPools]);
 134 | 
 135 |   const activePool =
 136 |     pools.find((p) => p.id === selectedPoolId) || pools[0];
 137 | 
 138 |   const handleSelectUser = async (user: Participant) => {
 139 |     setActiveUser(user);
 140 |     await PoolRepository.saveActiveUser(user);
 141 |   };
 142 | 
 143 |   const handleConfigSaved = async (newConfig: SupabaseConfig) => {
 144 |     await PoolRepository.saveSupabaseConfig(newConfig);
 145 |     await refreshPools();
 146 |     if (newConfig.enabled) {
 147 |       PoolRepository.subscribeToRealtime((table) => {
 148 |         refreshPools(table);
 149 |       });
 150 |     }
 151 |   };
 152 | 
 153 |   const handleSharePoolInvite = async () => {
 154 |     try {
 155 |       const message = buildShareablePoolInviteText(activePool);
 156 |       await Share.share({
 157 |         title: `Join ${activePool.title} (${activePool.joinCode}) on PoolCart`,
 158 |         message,
 159 |       });
 160 |     } catch (err) {
 161 |       console.warn('Share error:', err);
 162 |     }
 163 |   };
 164 | 
 165 |   const handleUpdateQuantity = async (itemId: string, delta: number) => {
 166 |     if (activePool.status === 'locked') return;
 167 | 
 168 |     let nextQuantityForSync = 0;
 169 | 
 170 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 171 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 172 | 
 173 |       const hasParticipant = pool.participants.some(
 174 |         (p) => p.id === activeUser.id
 175 |       );
 176 |       const updatedParticipants = hasParticipant
 177 |         ? pool.participants
 178 |         : [...pool.participants, activeUser];
 179 | 
 180 |       const updatedItems = pool.items.map((item) => {
 181 |         if (item.id !== itemId) return item;
 182 | 
 183 |         const existingClaim = item.claims.find(
 184 |           (c) => c.userId === activeUser.id
 185 |         );
 186 |         let newClaims = [...item.claims];
 187 | 
 188 |         if (existingClaim) {
 189 |           const nextQty = Math.max(0, existingClaim.quantity + delta);
 190 |           nextQuantityForSync = nextQty;
 191 |           if (nextQty === 0) {
 192 |             newClaims = newClaims.filter((c) => c.userId !== activeUser.id);
 193 |           } else {
 194 |             newClaims = newClaims.map((c) =>
 195 |               c.userId === activeUser.id ? { ...c, quantity: nextQty } : c
 196 |             );
 197 |           }
 198 |         } else if (delta > 0) {
 199 |           nextQuantityForSync = delta;
 200 |           newClaims.push({
 201 |             userId: activeUser.id,
 202 |             userName: activeUser.name,
 203 |             avatarColor: activeUser.avatarColor,
 204 |             quantity: delta,
 205 |           });
 206 |         }
 207 | 
 208 |         return { ...item, claims: newClaims };
 209 |       });
 210 | 
 211 |       return {
 212 |         ...pool,
 213 |         participants: updatedParticipants,
 214 |         items: updatedItems,
 215 |       };
 216 |     });
 217 | 
 218 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 219 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 220 | 
 221 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 222 |       const { error } = await PoolRepository.syncClaimQuantity(
 223 |         activePool.id,
 224 |         itemId,
 225 |         activeUser,
 226 |         nextQuantityForSync
 227 |       );
 228 |       if (error) {
 229 |         setSyncStatus((prev) => ({
 230 |           ...prev,
 231 |           mode: 'supabase_error',
 232 |           errorMessage: error,
 233 |         }));
 234 |       } else {
 235 |         setSyncStatus((prev) => ({
 236 |           ...prev,
 237 |           mode: 'supabase_live',
 238 |           lastSyncedAt: formatTimestamp(),
 239 |         }));
 240 |       }
 241 |     }
 242 |   };
 243 | 
 244 |   const handleSendMessage = async (text: string, isAnnouncement: boolean) => {
 245 |     const newMsg: PoolMessage = {
 246 |       id: `msg_${Date.now()}`,
 247 |       poolId: activePool.id,
 248 |       userId: activeUser.id,
 249 |       userName: activeUser.name,
 250 |       avatarColor: activeUser.avatarColor,
 251 |       isHost: Boolean(activeUser.isHost),
 252 |       isAnnouncement,
 253 |       text,
 254 |       createdAt: formatTimestamp(),
 255 |     };
 256 | 
 257 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 258 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 259 |       return {
 260 |         ...pool,
 261 |         messages: [...(pool.messages || []), newMsg],
 262 |       };
 263 |     });
 264 | 
 265 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 266 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 267 | 
 268 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 269 |       await PoolRepository.syncNewMessage(newMsg);
 270 |     }
 271 |   };
 272 | 
 273 |   /**
 274 |    * 1-Tap Rerun Weekly Pool: Clones the current pool's wholesale catalog items with fresh 0 claims
 275 |    * and generates a new invite code for next week's run.
 276 |    */
 277 |   const handleRerunWeeklyPool = async () => {
 278 |     const nowTs = Date.now();
 279 |     const prefix = activePool.joinCode.split('-')[0] || 'POOL';
 280 |     const nextCode = `${prefix}-${Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 89)}`;
 281 | 
 282 |     const clonedItems: CatalogItem[] = activePool.items.map((item, idx) => ({
 283 |       ...item,
 284 |       id: `item_${nowTs}_${idx}`,
 285 |       claims:
 286 |         idx === 0
 287 |           ? [
 288 |               {
 289 |                 userId: activeUser.id,
 290 |                 userName: activeUser.name,
 291 |                 avatarColor: activeUser.avatarColor,
 292 |                 quantity: 1,
 293 |               },
 294 |             ]
 295 |           : [],
 296 |     }));
 297 | 
 298 |     const rerunPool: GroupBuyPool = {
 299 |       ...activePool,
 300 |       id: `pool_${nowTs}`,
 301 |       joinCode: nextCode,
 302 |       title: `${activePool.title.replace(/ \(Next Week\)$/, '')} (Next Week)`,
 303 |       status: 'open',
 304 |       participants: [
 305 |         {
 306 |           ...activeUser,
 307 |           isHost: true,
 308 |           paidStatus: 'paid',
 309 |           pickedUp: false,
 310 |         },
 311 |       ],
 312 |       items: clonedItems,
 313 |       messages: [
 314 |         {
 315 |           id: `msg_${nowTs}`,
 316 |           poolId: `pool_${nowTs}`,
 317 |           userId: activeUser.id,
 318 |           userName: activeUser.name,
 319 |           avatarColor: activeUser.avatarColor,
 320 |           isHost: true,
 321 |           isAnnouncement: true,
 322 |           text: `New weekly run launched from our ${activePool.supplierName} catalog! Invite code: ${nextCode}`,
 323 |           createdAt: formatTimestamp(),
 324 |         },
 325 |       ],
 326 |     };
 327 | 
 328 |     const updatedPools = [rerunPool, ...pools];
 329 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 330 |     setSelectedPoolId(rerunPool.id);
 331 |     setActiveTab('catalog');
 332 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 333 | 
 334 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 335 |       await PoolRepository.syncNewPool(rerunPool);
 336 |     }
 337 |   };
 338 | 
 339 |   const handleToggleLockPool = async () => {
 340 |     const nextStatus: 'open' | 'locked' =
 341 |       activePool.status === 'locked' ? 'open' : 'locked';
 342 | 
 343 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) =>
 344 |       pool.id === activePool.id ? { ...pool, status: nextStatus } : pool
 345 |     );

 352 |     }

 355 |   const handleHostCoverRemainder = async (
 356 |     itemId: string,
 357 |     neededQty: number
 358 |   ) => {
 359 |     const hostParticipant =
 360 |       activePool.participants.find((p) => p.isHost) || activeUser;
 361 | 
 362 |     let syncedQty = neededQty;
 363 | 
 364 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 365 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 366 | 
 367 |       const updatedItems = pool.items.map((item) => {
 368 |         if (item.id !== itemId) return item;
 369 |         const existing = item.claims.find(
 370 |           (c) => c.userId === hostParticipant.id
 371 |         );
 372 |         let newClaims = [...item.claims];
 373 |         if (existing) {
 374 |           syncedQty = existing.quantity + neededQty;
 375 |           newClaims = newClaims.map((c) =>
 376 |             c.userId === hostParticipant.id ? { ...c, quantity: syncedQty } : c
 377 |           );
 378 |         } else {
 379 |           syncedQty = neededQty;
 380 |           newClaims.push({
 381 |             userId: hostParticipant.id,
 382 |             userName: hostParticipant.name,
 383 |             avatarColor: hostParticipant.avatarColor,
 384 |             quantity: neededQty,
 385 |           });
 386 |         }
 387 |         return { ...item, claims: newClaims };
 388 |       });
 389 | 
 390 |       return { ...pool, items: updatedItems };
 391 |     });
 392 | 
 393 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 394 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 395 | 
 396 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 397 |       await PoolRepository.syncClaimQuantity(
 398 |         activePool.id,
 399 |         itemId,
 400 |         hostParticipant,
 401 |         syncedQty
 402 |       );
 403 |     }
 404 |   };
 405 | 
 406 |   const handleTrimPartialCase = async (itemId: string) => {
 407 |     let trimmedClaimsForSync: ParticipantShare[] = [];
 408 | 
 409 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 410 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 411 | 
 412 |       const updatedItems = pool.items.map((item) => {
 413 |         if (item.id !== itemId) return item;
 414 |         const status = getCaseSplitStatus(item);
 415 |         if (status.remainderUnits === 0) return item;
 416 | 
 417 |         let toRemove = status.remainderUnits;
 418 |         const reversed = [...item.claims].reverse();
 419 |         const adjustedReversed: ParticipantShare[] = [];
 420 | 
 421 |         for (const claim of reversed) {
 422 |           if (toRemove <= 0) {
 423 |             adjustedReversed.push(claim);
 424 |           } else if (claim.quantity <= toRemove) {
 425 |             toRemove -= claim.quantity;
 426 |           } else {
 427 |             adjustedReversed.push({
 428 |               ...claim,
 429 |               quantity: claim.quantity - toRemove,
 430 |             });
 431 |             toRemove = 0;
 432 |           }
 433 |         }
 434 | 
 435 |         trimmedClaimsForSync = adjustedReversed.reverse();
 436 |         return { ...item, claims: trimmedClaimsForSync };
 437 |       });
 438 | 
 439 |       return { ...pool, items: updatedItems };
 440 |     });
 441 | 
 442 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 443 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 444 | 
 445 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 446 |       await PoolRepository.syncItemClaimsList(
 447 |         activePool.id,
 448 |         itemId,
 449 |         trimmedClaimsForSync
 450 |       );
 451 |     }
 452 |   };
 453 | 
 454 |   const handleSimulateNeighborAction = async () => {
 455 |     const otherNeighbors = DEMO_PROFILES.filter((p) => p.id !== activeUser.id);
 456 |     const randomNeighbor =
 457 |       otherNeighbors[Math.floor(Math.random() * otherNeighbors.length)] ||
 458 |       DEMO_PROFILES[1];
 459 | 
 460 |     const targetItem =
 461 |       activePool.items.find((i) => i.mode === 'case_split') ||
 462 |       activePool.items[0];
 463 |     if (!targetItem) return;
 464 | 
 465 |     let simulatedNextQty = 1;
 466 | 
 467 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 468 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 469 | 
 470 |       const hasNeighbor = pool.participants.some(
 471 |         (p) => p.id === randomNeighbor.id
 472 |       );
 473 |       const updatedParticipants = hasNeighbor
 474 |         ? pool.participants
 475 |         : [...pool.participants, randomNeighbor];
 476 | 
 477 |       const updatedItems = pool.items.map((item) => {
 478 |         if (item.id !== targetItem.id) return item;
 479 |         const existing = item.claims.find(
 480 |           (c) => c.userId === randomNeighbor.id
 481 |         );
 482 |         let newClaims = [...item.claims];
 483 |         if (existing) {
 484 |           simulatedNextQty = existing.quantity + 1;
 485 |           newClaims = newClaims.map((c) =>
 486 |             c.userId === randomNeighbor.id
 487 |               ? { ...c, quantity: simulatedNextQty }
 488 |               : c
 489 |           );
 490 |         } else {
 491 |           simulatedNextQty = 1;
 492 |           newClaims.push({
 493 |             userId: randomNeighbor.id,
 494 |             userName: randomNeighbor.name,
 495 |             avatarColor: randomNeighbor.avatarColor,
 496 |             quantity: 1,
 497 |           });
 498 |         }
 499 |         return { ...item, claims: newClaims };
 500 |       });
 501 | 
 502 |       return {
 503 |         ...pool,
 504 |         participants: updatedParticipants,
 505 |         items: updatedItems,
 506 |       };
 507 |     });
 508 | 
 509 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 510 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 511 | 
 512 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 513 |       await PoolRepository.syncClaimQuantity(
 514 |         activePool.id,
 515 |         targetItem.id,
 516 |         randomNeighbor,
 517 |         simulatedNextQty
 518 |       );
 519 |     }
 520 | 
 521 |     setSyncStatus((prev) => ({
 522 |       ...prev,
 523 |       lastSyncedAt: formatTimestamp(),
 524 |       lastRealtimeEvent: `${randomNeighbor.name.split(' ')[0]} claimed +1 ${targetItem.emoji}`,
 525 |     }));
 526 |   };
 527 | 
 528 |   const handleAddItem = async (newItem: CatalogItem) => {
 529 |     const updatedPools = pools.map((pool) => {
 530 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 531 |       const hasParticipant = pool.participants.some(
 532 |         (p) => p.id === activeUser.id
 533 |       );
 534 |       return {
 535 |         ...pool,
 536 |         participants: hasParticipant
 537 |           ? pool.participants
 538 |           : [...pool.participants, activeUser],
 539 |         items: [newItem, ...pool.items],
 540 |       };
 541 |     });
 542 | 
 543 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 544 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 545 | 
 546 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 547 |       await PoolRepository.syncNewItem(activePool.id, newItem, activeUser);
 548 |     }
 549 |   };
 550 | 
 551 |   const handleCreatePool = async (newPool: GroupBuyPool) => {
 552 |     const updatedPools = [newPool, ...pools];
 553 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 554 |     setSelectedPoolId(newPool.id);
 555 |     setActiveTab('catalog');
 556 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 557 | 
 558 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 559 |       await PoolRepository.syncNewPool(newPool);
 560 |     }
 561 |   };
 562 | 
 563 |   const handleTogglePaidStatus = async (userId: string) => {
 564 |     let nextStatus: 'pending' | 'paid' = 'paid';
 565 | 
 566 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 567 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 568 |       return {
 569 |         ...pool,
 570 |         participants: pool.participants.map((p) => {
 571 |           if (p.id !== userId) return p;
 572 |           nextStatus = p.paidStatus === 'paid' ? 'pending' : 'paid';
 573 |           return {
 574 |             ...p,
 575 |             paidStatus: nextStatus,
 576 |           };
 577 |         }),
 578 |       };
 579 |     });
 580 | 
 581 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 582 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 583 | 
 584 |     if (PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig().enabled) {
 585 |       await PoolRepository.syncPaidStatus(activePool.id, userId, nextStatus);
 586 |     }
 587 |   };
 588 | 
 589 |   const handleTogglePickedUp = async (userId: string) => {
 590 |     const updatedPools: GroupBuyPool[] = pools.map((pool) => {
 591 |       if (pool.id !== activePool.id) return pool;
 592 |       return {
 593 |         ...pool,
 594 |         participants: pool.participants.map((p) =>
 595 |           p.id === userId ? { ...p, pickedUp: !p.pickedUp } : p
 596 |         ),
 597 |       };
 598 |     });
 599 | 
 600 |     setPools(updatedPools);
 601 |     await PoolRepository.saveLocalPools(updatedPools);
 602 |   };
 603 | 
 604 |   const handleResetDemo = async () => {
 605 |     const fresh = await PoolRepository.resetDemoData();
 606 |     setPools(fresh);
 607 |     setSelectedPoolId(fresh[0].id);
 608 |   };
 609 | 
 610 |   const filteredItems = activePool.items.filter((i) =>
 611 |     itemFilter === 'all' ? true : i.mode === itemFilter
 612 |   );
 613 | 
 614 |   const myBreakdown = getParticipantBreakdowns(activePool).find(
 615 |     (b) => b.userId === activeUser.id
 616 |   );
 617 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(activePool);
 618 |   const messageCount = (activePool.messages || []).length;
 619 | 
 620 |   return (
 621 |     <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
 622 |       <StatusBar barStyle="dark-content" />
 623 | 
 624 |       {/* Header */}
 625 |       <View style={styles.header}>
 626 |         <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 627 |           <View style={styles.brandRow}>
 628 |             <Text style={styles.brandTitle}>🥬 PoolCart</Text>
 629 |             <TouchableOpacity
 630 |               style={[
 631 |                 styles.syncPill,
 632 |                 syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live'
 633 |                   ? styles.syncPillLive
 634 |                   : styles.syncPillLocal,
 635 |               ]}
 636 |               onPress={() => setActiveTab('architecture')}
 637 |             >
 638 |               <View
 639 |                 style={[
 640 |                   styles.syncDot,
 641 |                   syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live'
 642 |                     ? { backgroundColor: '#10B981' }
 643 |                     : { backgroundColor: '#0D9488' },
 644 |                 ]}
 645 |               />
 646 |               <Text style={styles.syncPillText}>
 647 |                 {syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live' ? 'Live' : 'Local'}
 648 |               </Text>
 649 |             </TouchableOpacity>
 650 |           </View>
 651 |           <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setActiveTab('architecture')}>
 652 |             <Text style={styles.locationSub}>
 653 |               👤{' '}
 654 |               <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: '#0F172A' }}>
 655 |                 {activeUser.name}
 656 |               </Text>{' '}
 657 |               • Code:{' '}
 658 |               <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800', color: '#0D9488' }}>
 659 |                 {activePool.joinCode}
 660 |               </Text>
 661 |             </Text>
 662 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 663 |         </View>
 664 | 
 665 |         <View style={styles.headerActions}>
 666 |           <TouchableOpacity
 667 |             style={styles.codeJoinButton}
 668 |             onPress={() => setShowJoinByCodeModal(true)}
 669 |           >
 670 |             <Text style={styles.codeJoinButtonText}>🔑 Code</Text>
 671 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 672 | 
 673 |           <TouchableOpacity
 674 |             style={styles.sharePoolButton}
 675 |             onPress={handleSharePoolInvite}
 676 |           >
 677 |             <Text style={styles.sharePoolButtonText}>📤 Invite</Text>
 678 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 679 | 
 680 |           <TouchableOpacity
 681 |             style={styles.newPoolButton}
 682 |             onPress={() => setShowCreatePoolModal(true)}
 683 |           >
 684 |             <Text style={styles.newPoolButtonText}>+ Host</Text>
 685 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 686 |         </View>
 687 |       </View>
 688 | 
 689 |       {/* Horizontal Pool Selector */}
 690 |       <View style={styles.poolSelectorWrap}>
 691 |         <ScrollView
 692 |           horizontal
 693 |           showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
 694 |           contentContainerStyle={styles.poolScrollContent}
 695 |         >
 696 |           {pools.map((pool) => {
 697 |             const isSelected = pool.id === activePool.id;
 698 |             const poolSummary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
 699 |             return (
 700 |               <TouchableOpacity
 701 |                 key={pool.id}
 702 |                 style={[
 703 |                   styles.poolPill,
 704 |                   isSelected && styles.poolPillSelected,
 705 |                 ]}
 706 |                 onPress={() => setSelectedPoolId(pool.id)}
 707 |               >
 708 |                 <Text
 709 |                   style={[
 710 |                     styles.poolPillTitle,
 711 |                     isSelected && styles.poolPillTitleSelected,
 712 |                   ]}
 713 |                   numberOfLines={1}
 714 |                 >
 715 |                   {pool.status === 'locked' ? '🔒 ' : ''}
 716 |                   {pool.title}
 717 |                 </Text>
 718 |                 <Text
 719 |                   style={[
 720 |                     styles.poolPillMeta,
 721 |                     isSelected && styles.poolPillMetaSelected,
 722 |                   ]}
 723 |                 >
 724 |                   [{pool.joinCode}] ${poolSummary.combinedPoolTotal.toFixed(0)} / $
 725 |                   {pool.minimumOrderAmount} min
 726 |                 </Text>
 727 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 728 |             );
 729 |           })}
 730 |         </ScrollView>
 731 |       </View>
 732 | 
 733 |       {/* Main Content */}
 734 |       <ScrollView
 735 |         style={styles.mainScroll}

 737 |       >

 740 |           <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 741 |             <Text style={styles.supplierBadgeText}>

 743 |                 ? '🔒 POOL LOCKED • READY FOR ORDER & PICKUP'

 755 |

 758 |           <TouchableOpacity
 759 |             style={[

 762 |             ]}
 763 |             onPress={() => setActiveTab('catalog')}
 764 |           >
 765 |             <Text
 766 |               style={[
 767 |                 styles.tabButtonText,
 768 |                 activeTab === 'catalog' && styles.tabButtonTextActive,
 769 |               ]}

 772 |             </Text>

 789 |             </Text>

 867 |                         itemFilter === f.key && styles.filterChipTextActive,
 868 |                       ]}
 869 |                     >
 870 |                       {f.label}
 871 |                     </Text>

 873 |                 ))}

 894 |               />
 895 |             ))}

 897 |         )}
 898 |

 903 |             onTogglePaidStatus={handleTogglePaidStatus}
 904 |           />
 905 |         )}
 906 |

 911 |             onSendMessage={handleSendMessage}
 912 |           />
 913 |         )}
 914 |

 921 |             onTrimPartialCase={handleTrimPartialCase}

 925 |         )}
 926 |

 929 |             activeUser={activeUser}
 930 |             onSelectUser={handleSelectUser}
 931 |             syncStatus={syncStatus}
 932 |             onConfigSaved={handleConfigSaved}

 935 |           />
 936 |         )}
 937 |       </ScrollView>

 946 |             </Text>

 950 |               </Text>

 952 |                 {' '}
 953 |                 • Saving ${myBreakdown.totalEstimatedSavings.toFixed(2)}
 954 |               </Text>
 955 |             </View>
 956 |           </View>
 957 |

 959 |             style={styles.viewSplitButton}
 960 |             onPress={() =>
 961 |               setActiveTab(activeTab === 'splits' ? 'catalog' : 'splits')
 962 |             }
 963 |           >
 964 |             <Text style={styles.viewSplitButtonText}>
 965 |               {activeTab === 'splits'
 966 |                 ? 'Back to Items'
 967 |                 : summary.isMinimumMet
 968 |                 ? 'Pay / Split ✓'
 969 |                 : 'View Split Ledger'}
 970 |             </Text>
 971 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 972 |         </View>
 973 |       )}
 974 | 
 975 |       {/* Modals */}
 976 |       <AddItemModal
 977 |         visible={showAddItemModal}

 979 |         onClose={() => setShowAddItemModal(false)}
 980 |         onAddItem={handleAddItem}
 981 |       />
 982 |

 988 |       />

 993 |         onClose={() => setShowJoinByCodeModal(false)}
 994 |         onSelectPoolId={(poolId) => {
 995 |           setSelectedPoolId(poolId);
 996 |           setActiveTab('catalog');
 997 |         }}
 998 |       />
 999 |     </SafeAreaView>
1000 |   );
1001 | }

===== FILE: src/services/notifications.ts =====

  11 | 
  12 | const STORAGE_KEY_ALERTS = '@poolcart_alerts_v1';
  13 | const STORAGE_KEY_PREFS = '@poolcart_notif_prefs_v1';
  14 | 
  15 | export const DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS: NotificationPreferences = {
  16 |   cutoffReminder2h: true,
  17 |   freeDeliveryProximity: true,

  19 |   porchDropArrival: true,

  45 | function nowTimeLabel(): string {
  46 |   const now = new Date();
  47 |   return now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  48 | }
  49 | 
  50 | export const NotificationService = {
  51 |   async loadAlerts(): Promise<PoolNotificationAlert[]> {
  52 |     try {
  53 |       const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_ALERTS);
  54 |       if (!raw) {
  55 |         await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_ALERTS, JSON.stringify(INITIAL_ALERTS));
  56 |         return INITIAL_ALERTS;
  57 |       }
  58 |       return JSON.parse(raw) as PoolNotificationAlert[];
  59 |     } catch {
  60 |       return INITIAL_ALERTS;
  61 |     }
  62 |   },
  63 | 
  64 |   async saveAlerts(alerts: PoolNotificationAlert[]): Promise<void> {
  65 |     try {
  66 |       await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_ALERTS, JSON.stringify(alerts.slice(0, 40)));
  67 |     } catch (err) {
  68 |       console.warn('Failed to save alerts:', err);
  69 |     }
  70 |   },
  71 | 
  72 |   async loadPreferences(): Promise<NotificationPreferences> {
  73 |     try {
  74 |       const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY_PREFS);
  75 |       if (!raw) return DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS;
  76 |       return { ...DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  77 |     } catch {
  78 |       return DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_PREFS;
  79 |     }
  80 |   },
  81 | 
  82 |   async savePreferences(prefs: NotificationPreferences): Promise<void> {
  83 |     try {
  84 |       await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_PREFS, JSON.stringify(prefs));
  85 |     } catch (err) {
  86 |       console.warn('Failed to save notification prefs:', err);
  87 |     }
  88 |   },
  89 | 
  90 |   createAlert(
  91 |     pool: GroupBuyPool,
  92 |     category: AlertCategory,
  93 |     title: string,
  94 |     body: string
  95 |   ): PoolNotificationAlert {
  96 |     return {
  97 |       id: `alert_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
  98 |       poolId: pool.id,
  99 |       poolTitle: pool.title,
 100 |       category,
 101 |       title,
 102 |       body,
 103 |       createdAt: nowTimeLabel(),
 104 |       read: false,
 105 |     };
 106 |   },
 107 | 
 108 |   /**
 109 |    * Evaluates pool & case-split threshold transitions after an atomic claim update
 110 |    * and returns any newly triggered alerts (e.g., Case Locked, Minimum Met, <=$25 to Free Delivery, Free Delivery Unlocked).
 111 |    */
 112 |   evaluateClaimMilestones(
 113 |     prevPool: GroupBuyPool,
 114 |     nextPool: GroupBuyPool,
 115 |     item: CatalogItem,
 116 |     claimResult: AtomicClaimResult,
 117 |     prefs: NotificationPreferences
 118 |   ): PoolNotificationAlert[] {
 119 |     const newAlerts: PoolNotificationAlert[] = [];
 120 |     const prevSummary = getPoolProgressSummary(prevPool);
 121 |     const nextSummary = getPoolProgressSummary(nextPool);
 122 | 
 123 |     // 1. Bulk Case Completion or Rollover
 124 |     if (prefs.caseSplitCompletion) {
 125 |       if (claimResult.cappedAtMaxCases) {
 126 |         newAlerts.push(
 127 |           this.createAlert(
 128 |             nextPool,
 129 |             'case_locked',
 130 |             `🛑 Max Case Cap Reached: ${item.emoji} ${item.name}`,
 131 |             `Host capped this bulk item at ${item.maxCases} full case(s) (${claimResult.totalClaimedUnits} units total).`
 132 |           )
 133 |         );
 134 |       } else if (claimResult.justCompletedCase) {
 135 |         newAlerts.push(
 136 |           this.createAlert(
 137 |             nextPool,
 138 |             'case_locked',
 139 |             `📦 Case #${claimResult.fullCasesCount} Locked: ${item.emoji} ${item.name}!`,
 140 |             `All ${item.caseSize}/${item.caseSize} shares claimed! Full wholesale case is now locked into the supplier order.`
 141 |           )
 142 |         );
 143 |       } else if (claimResult.justOpenedNewCase) {
 144 |         newAlerts.push(
 145 |           this.createAlert(
 146 |             nextPool,
 147 |             'case_locked',
 148 |             `🆕 Case #${claimResult.fullCasesCount + 1} Opened: ${item.emoji} ${item.name}`,
 149 |             `Case #${claimResult.fullCasesCount} is full! Started Case #${claimResult.fullCasesCount + 1} (1/${item.caseSize} claimed).`
 150 |           )
 151 |         );
 152 |       }
 153 |     }
 154 | 
 155 |     // 2. Wholesale Minimum Order Met
 156 |     if (!prevSummary.isMinimumMet && nextSummary.isMinimumMet) {
 157 |       newAlerts.push(
 158 |         this.createAlert(
 159 |           nextPool,
 160 |           'minimum_met',
 161 |           `🎯 Wholesale Minimum Met ($${nextPool.minimumOrderAmount}+)!`,
 162 |           `${nextPool.title} reached $${nextSummary.combinedPoolTotal.toFixed(2)}! This week's wholesale delivery is officially a go.`
 163 |         )
 164 |       );
 165 |     }
 166 | 
 167 |     // 3. Free Delivery Unlocked or Within $30 Proximity
 168 |     if (prefs.freeDeliveryProximity) {
 169 |       if (
 170 |         !prevSummary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked &&
 171 |         nextSummary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked
 172 |       ) {
 173 |         newAlerts.push(
 174 |           this.createAlert(
 175 |             nextPool,
 176 |             'free_delivery_unlocked',
 177 |             `🎉 FREE Wholesale Delivery Unlocked!`,
 178 |             `${nextPool.title} passed $${nextPool.freeDeliveryThreshold}! The $${nextPool.deliveryFee} supplier delivery fee is now $0.00 for all neighbors.`
 179 |           )
 180 |         );
 181 |       } else if (
 182 |         !nextSummary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked &&
 183 |         prevSummary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery > 30 &&
 184 |         nextSummary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery <= 30 &&
 185 |         nextSummary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery > 0
 186 |       ) {
 187 |         newAlerts.push(
 188 |           this.createAlert(
 189 |             nextPool,
 190 |             'free_delivery_near',
 191 |             `🚚 Only $${nextSummary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery.toFixed(2)} Away from Free Delivery!`,
 192 |             `Add $${nextSummary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery.toFixed(2)} more to ${nextPool.title} to waive the $${nextPool.deliveryFee} delivery fee for everyone.`
 193 |           )
 194 |         );
 195 |       }
 196 |     }
 197 | 
 198 |     return newAlerts;
 199 |   },
 200 | };

  68 | function getOrCreateSupabaseClient(): SupabaseClient | null {
  69 |   if (!currentConfig.enabled || !currentConfig.url || !currentConfig.anonKey) {
  70 |     return null;
  71 |   }
  72 |   if (!activeClient) {
  73 |     activeClient = createClient(currentConfig.url.trim(), currentConfig.anonKey.trim(), {
  74 |       auth: {
  75 |         storage: AsyncStorage,
  76 |         autoRefreshToken: true,
  77 |         persistSession: true,
  78 |         detectSessionInUrl: false,
  79 |       },
  80 |     });
  81 |   }
  82 |   return activeClient;
  83 | }
  84 | 
  85 | /**
  86 |  * Local in-memory / AsyncStorage equivalent of the Postgres `claim_item_share` RPC:
  87 |  * Enforces `maxCases` cap, computes whether a case just locked (`justCompletedCase`)
  88 |  * or rolled over into a new case (`justOpenedNewCase`).
  89 |  */
  90 | export function evaluateAtomicClaimTransition(
  91 |   item: CatalogItem,
  92 |   user: Participant,
  93 |   delta: number
  94 | ): {
  95 |   updatedClaims: ParticipantShare[];
  96 |   result: AtomicClaimResult;
  97 | } {
  98 |   const existingUserClaim = item.claims.find((c) => c.userId === user.id);
  99 |   const currentUserQty = existingUserClaim ? existingUserClaim.quantity : 0;
 100 |   const otherUsersQty = item.claims
 101 |     .filter((c) => c.userId !== user.id)
 102 |     .reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.quantity, 0);
 103 | 
 104 |   const prevTotal = currentUserQty + otherUsersQty;
 105 |   let nextUserQty = Math.max(0, currentUserQty + delta);
 106 |   let nextTotal = otherUsersQty + nextUserQty;
 107 |   let cappedAtMaxCases = false;
 108 | 
 109 |   const caseSize = item.mode === 'case_split' && item.caseSize ? item.caseSize : 1;
 110 | 
 111 |   if (
 112 |     item.mode === 'case_split' &&
 113 |     item.maxCases &&
 114 |     item.maxCases > 0
 115 |   ) {
 116 |     const maxAllowedUnits = item.maxCases * caseSize;
 117 |     if (nextTotal > maxAllowedUnits) {
 118 |       nextUserQty = Math.max(0, maxAllowedUnits - otherUsersQty);
 119 |       nextTotal = otherUsersQty + nextUserQty;
 120 |       cappedAtMaxCases = true;
 121 |     }
 122 |   }
 123 | 
 124 |   let updatedClaims = [...item.claims];
 125 |   if (nextUserQty <= 0) {
 126 |     updatedClaims = updatedClaims.filter((c) => c.userId !== user.id);
 127 |   } else if (existingUserClaim) {
 128 |     updatedClaims = updatedClaims.map((c) =>
 129 |       c.userId === user.id ? { ...c, quantity: nextUserQty } : c
 130 |     );
 131 |   } else {
 132 |     updatedClaims.push({
 133 |       userId: user.id,
 134 |       userName: user.name,
 135 |       avatarColor: user.avatarColor,
 136 |       quantity: nextUserQty,
 137 |     });
 138 |   }
 139 | 
 140 |   const prevFullCases =
 141 |     item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1
 142 |       ? Math.floor(prevTotal / caseSize)
 143 |       : prevTotal;
 144 |   const nextFullCases =
 145 |     item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1
 146 |       ? Math.floor(nextTotal / caseSize)
 147 |       : nextTotal;
 148 |   const remainderUnits =
 149 |     item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1 ? nextTotal % caseSize : 0;
 150 | 
 151 |   const justCompletedCase =
 152 |     item.mode === 'case_split' &&
 153 |     caseSize > 1 &&
 154 |     nextFullCases > prevFullCases &&
 155 |     remainderUnits === 0;
 156 | 
 157 |   const justOpenedNewCase =
 158 |     item.mode === 'case_split' &&
 159 |     caseSize > 1 &&
 160 |     prevTotal > 0 &&
 161 |     prevTotal % caseSize === 0 &&
 162 |     nextTotal > prevTotal;
 163 | 
 164 |   return {
 165 |     updatedClaims,
 166 |     result: {
 167 |       newUserQuantity: nextUserQty,
 168 |       totalClaimedUnits: nextTotal,
 169 |       fullCasesCount: nextFullCases,
 170 |       remainderUnits,
 171 |       justCompletedCase,
 172 |       justOpenedNewCase,
 173 |       cappedAtMaxCases,
 174 |     },
 175 |   };
 176 | }

 233 |   async loadPools(): Promise<{ pools: GroupBuyPool[]; error?: string }> {
 234 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 235 |     if (client) {
 236 |       try {
 237 |         const [poolsRes, partsRes, itemsRes, claimsRes, msgsRes] = await Promise.all([
 238 |           client.from('pools').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }),
 239 |           client.from('pool_participants').select('*'),
 240 |           client.from('catalog_items').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }),
 241 |           client.from('item_claims').select('*'),
 242 |           client.from('pool_messages').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true }),
 243 |         ]);
 244 | 
 245 |         if (poolsRes.error) throw poolsRes.error;
 246 |         if (partsRes.error) throw partsRes.error;
 247 |         if (itemsRes.error) throw itemsRes.error;
 248 |         if (claimsRes.error) throw claimsRes.error;
 249 | 
 250 |         if (!poolsRes.data || poolsRes.data.length === 0) {
 251 |           await this.seedSupabasePools(INITIAL_POOLS);
 252 |           await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_POOLS, JSON.stringify(INITIAL_POOLS));
 253 |           return { pools: INITIAL_POOLS };
 254 |         }
 255 | 
 256 |         const assembled: GroupBuyPool[] = poolsRes.data.map((row: any) => {
 257 |           const poolParticipants: Participant[] = (partsRes.data || [])
 258 |             .filter((p: any) => p.pool_id === row.id)
 259 |             .map((p: any) => ({
 260 |               id: p.user_id,
 261 |               name: p.user_name,
 262 |               neighborhood: p.neighborhood || 'Victoria Gardens',
 263 |               avatarColor: p.avatar_color || '#0D9488',
 264 |               isHost: Boolean(p.is_host),
 265 |               paidStatus: (p.paid_status === 'paid' ? 'paid' : 'pending') as 'pending' | 'paid',
 266 |               pickedUp: Boolean(p.picked_up),
 267 |             }));
 268 | 
 269 |           const poolItems: CatalogItem[] = (itemsRes.data || [])
 270 |             .filter((i: any) => i.pool_id === row.id)
 271 |             .map((i: any) => {
 272 |               const itemClaims: ParticipantShare[] = (claimsRes.data || [])
 273 |                 .filter((c: any) => c.item_id === i.id && Number(c.quantity) > 0)
 274 |                 .map((c: any) => ({
 275 |                   userId: c.user_id,
 276 |                   userName: c.user_name,
 277 |                   avatarColor: c.avatar_color,
 278 |                   quantity: Number(c.quantity),
 279 |                 }));
 280 | 
 281 |               return {
 282 |                 id: i.id,
 283 |                 name: i.name,
 284 |                 category: i.category,
 285 |                 emoji: i.emoji || '🛒',
 286 |                 mode: i.mode === 'case_split' ? 'case_split' : 'individual',
 287 |                 unitLabel: i.unit_label,
 288 |                 unitPrice: Number(i.unit_price),
 289 |                 retailPrice: Number(i.retail_price),
 290 |                 caseSize: i.case_size ? Number(i.case_size) : undefined,
 291 |                 maxCases: i.max_cases ? Number(i.max_cases) : undefined,
 292 |                 claims: itemClaims,
 293 |               };
 294 |             });
 295 | 
 296 |           const poolMessages: PoolMessage[] = (msgsRes.data || [])
 297 |             .filter((m: any) => m.pool_id === row.id)
 298 |             .map((m: any) => ({
 299 |               id: m.id,
 300 |               poolId: m.pool_id,
 301 |               userId: m.user_id,
 302 |               userName: m.user_name,
 303 |               avatarColor: m.avatar_color || '#0D9488',
 304 |               isHost: Boolean(m.is_host),
 305 |               isAnnouncement: Boolean(m.is_announcement),
 306 |               text: m.text,
 307 |               createdAt: m.created_at_label || 'Just now',
 308 |             }));
 309 | 
 310 |           return {
 311 |             id: row.id,
 312 |             joinCode: row.join_code || 'POOL-01',
 313 |             title: row.title,
 314 |             supplierName: row.supplier_name,
 315 |             supplierBadge: row.supplier_badge,
 316 |             category: row.category,
 317 |             hostName: row.host_name,
 318 |             hostVenmoHandle: row.host_venmo_handle || 'PoolCart-Host',
 319 |             neighborhood: row.neighborhood,
 320 |             pickupAddress: row.pickup_address,
 321 |             pickupWindow: row.pickup_window,
 322 |             cutoffTime: row.cutoff_time,
 323 |             minimumOrderAmount: Number(row.minimum_order_amount),
 324 |             deliveryFee: Number(row.delivery_fee),
 325 |             freeDeliveryThreshold: Number(row.free_delivery_threshold),
 326 |             status: row.status || 'open',
 327 |             participants: poolParticipants,
 328 |             items: poolItems,
 329 |             messages: poolMessages,
 330 |           };
 331 |         });
 332 |

 337 |         const fallback = await this.loadLocalPools();

 351 |         return INITIAL_POOLS;

 367 |   async seedSupabasePools(pools: GroupBuyPool[]): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
 368 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 369 |     if (!client) return {};
 370 | 
 371 |     try {
 372 |       const poolRows = pools.map((p) => ({
 373 |         id: p.id,
 374 |         join_code: p.joinCode,
 375 |         title: p.title,
 376 |         supplier_name: p.supplierName,
 377 |         supplier_badge: p.supplierBadge,
 378 |         category: p.category,
 379 |         host_name: p.hostName,
 380 |         host_venmo_handle: p.hostVenmoHandle ?? null,
 381 |         neighborhood: p.neighborhood,
 382 |         pickup_address: p.pickupAddress,
 383 |         pickup_window: p.pickupWindow,
 384 |         cutoff_time: p.cutoffTime,
 385 |         minimum_order_amount: p.minimumOrderAmount,
 386 |         delivery_fee: p.deliveryFee,
 387 |         free_delivery_threshold: p.freeDeliveryThreshold,
 388 |         status: p.status,
 389 |       }));
 390 | 
 391 |       const participantRows = pools.flatMap((p) =>
 392 |         p.participants.map((part) => ({
 393 |           pool_id: p.id,
 394 |           user_id: part.id,
 395 |           user_name: part.name,
 396 |           neighborhood: part.neighborhood,
 397 |           avatar_color: part.avatarColor,
 398 |           is_host: Boolean(part.isHost),
 399 |           paid_status: part.paidStatus,
 400 |           picked_up: Boolean(part.pickedUp),
 401 |         }))
 402 |       );
 403 | 
 404 |       const itemRows = pools.flatMap((p) =>
 405 |         p.items.map((item) => ({
 406 |           id: item.id,
 407 |           pool_id: p.id,
 408 |           name: item.name,
 409 |           category: item.category,
 410 |           emoji: item.emoji,
 411 |           mode: item.mode,
 412 |           unit_label: item.unitLabel,
 413 |           unit_price: item.unitPrice,
 414 |           retail_price: item.retailPrice,
 415 |           case_size: item.caseSize ?? null,
 416 |           max_cases: item.maxCases ?? null,
 417 |         }))
 418 |       );
 419 | 
 420 |       const claimRows = pools.flatMap((p) =>
 421 |         p.items.flatMap((item) =>
 422 |           item.claims.map((c) => ({
 423 |             pool_id: p.id,
 424 |             item_id: item.id,
 425 |             user_id: c.userId,
 426 |             user_name: c.userName,
 427 |             avatar_color: c.avatarColor,
 428 |             quantity: c.quantity,
 429 |           }))
 430 |         )
 431 |       );
 432 | 
 433 |       const messageRows = pools.flatMap((p) =>
 434 |         (p.messages || []).map((m) => ({
 435 |           id: m.id,
 436 |           pool_id: p.id,
 437 |           user_id: m.userId,
 438 |           user_name: m.userName,
 439 |           avatar_color: m.avatarColor,
 440 |           is_host: Boolean(m.isHost),
 441 |           is_announcement: Boolean(m.isAnnouncement),
 442 |           text: m.text,
 443 |           created_at_label: m.createdAt,
 444 |         }))
 445 |       );
 446 | 
 447 |       const r1 = await client.from('pools').upsert(poolRows, { onConflict: 'id' });
 448 |       if (r1.error) throw r1.error;
 449 | 
 450 |       const r2 = await client
 451 |         .from('pool_participants')
 452 |         .upsert(participantRows, { onConflict: 'pool_id,user_id' });
 453 |       if (r2.error) throw r2.error;
 454 | 
 455 |       const r3 = await client.from('catalog_items').upsert(itemRows, { onConflict: 'id' });
 456 |       if (r3.error) throw r3.error;
 457 | 
 458 |       if (claimRows.length > 0) {
 459 |         const r4 = await client
 460 |           .from('item_claims')
 461 |           .upsert(claimRows, { onConflict: 'item_id,user_id' });
 462 |         if (r4.error) throw r4.error;
 463 |       }
 464 | 
 465 |       if (messageRows.length > 0) {
 466 |         await client.from('pool_messages').upsert(messageRows, { onConflict: 'id' });
 467 |       }
 468 | 
 469 |       return {};
 470 |     } catch (err: any) {
 471 |       return { error: err?.message || 'Failed to seed Supabase database' };
 472 |     }
 473 |   },
 474 | 
 475 |   /**
 476 |    * Calls the PostgreSQL `claim_item_share` RPC (`FOR UPDATE` row-level lock) when
 477 |    * connected to Supabase, falling back to direct table upsert if the user hasn't run
 478 |    * the updated SQL function yet.
 479 |    */
 480 |   async syncAtomicClaimDelta(
 481 |     poolId: string,
 482 |     itemId: string,
 483 |     user: Participant,
 484 |     delta: number,
 485 |     fallbackQuantity: number
 486 |   ): Promise<{ rpcResult?: AtomicClaimResult; error?: string }> {
 487 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 488 |     if (!client) return {};
 489 | 
 490 |     try {
 491 |       const { data, error } = await client.rpc('claim_item_share', {
 492 |         p_pool_id: poolId,
 493 |         p_item_id: itemId,
 494 |         p_user_id: user.id,
 495 |         p_user_name: user.name,
 496 |         p_avatar_color: user.avatarColor,
 497 |         p_neighborhood: user.neighborhood,
 498 |         p_delta: delta,
 499 |       });
 500 | 
 501 |       if (!error && data) {
 502 |         return { rpcResult: data as AtomicClaimResult };
 503 |       }
 504 | 
 505 |       // Fallback to standard upsert if RPC isn't created yet
 506 |       return await this.syncClaimQuantity(poolId, itemId, user, fallbackQuantity);
 507 |     } catch (err: any) {
 508 |       return { error: err?.message || 'Atomic claim RPC failed' };
 509 |     }
 510 |   },
 511 | 
 512 |   async syncClaimQuantity(
 513 |     poolId: string,
 514 |     itemId: string,
 515 |     user: Participant,
 516 |     nextQuantity: number
 517 |   ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
 518 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 519 |     if (!client) return {};
 520 | 
 521 |     try {
 522 |       await client.from('pool_participants').upsert(
 523 |         {
 524 |           pool_id: poolId,
 525 |           user_id: user.id,
 526 |           user_name: user.name,
 527 |           neighborhood: user.neighborhood,
 528 |           avatar_color: user.avatarColor,
 529 |           is_host: Boolean(user.isHost),
 530 |           paid_status: user.paidStatus,
 531 |         },
 532 |         { onConflict: 'pool_id,user_id' }
 533 |       );
 534 | 
 535 |       if (nextQuantity <= 0) {
 536 |         const { error } = await client
 537 |           .from('item_claims')
 538 |           .delete()
 539 |           .match({ item_id: itemId, user_id: user.id });
 540 |         if (error) throw error;
 541 |       } else {
 542 |         const { error } = await client.from('item_claims').upsert(
 543 |           {
 544 |             pool_id: poolId,
 545 |             item_id: itemId,
 546 |             user_id: user.id,
 547 |             user_name: user.name,
 548 |             avatar_color: user.avatarColor,
 549 |             quantity: nextQuantity,
 550 |             updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
 551 |           },
 552 |           { onConflict: 'item_id,user_id' }
 553 |         );
 554 |         if (error) throw error;
 555 |       }
 556 |       return {};
 557 |     } catch (err: any) {
 558 |       return { error: err?.message || 'Failed to sync item claim' };
 559 |     }
 560 |   },
 561 | 
 562 |   async syncNewItem(
 563 |     poolId: string,
 564 |     item: CatalogItem,
 565 |     creator: Participant
 566 |   ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
 567 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 568 |     if (!client) return {};
 569 | 
 570 |     try {
 571 |       const { error: itemErr } = await client.from('catalog_items').insert({
 572 |         id: item.id,
 573 |         pool_id: poolId,
 574 |         name: item.name,
 575 |         category: item.category,
 576 |         emoji: item.emoji,
 577 |         mode: item.mode,
 578 |         unit_label: item.unitLabel,
 579 |         unit_price: item.unitPrice,
 580 |         retail_price: item.retailPrice,
 581 |         case_size: item.caseSize ?? null,
 582 |         max_cases: item.maxCases ?? null,
 583 |       });
 584 |       if (itemErr) throw itemErr;
 585 | 
 586 |       const firstClaim = item.claims[0];
 587 |       if (firstClaim) {
 588 |         await this.syncClaimQuantity(poolId, item.id, creator, firstClaim.quantity);
 589 |       }
 590 |       return {};
 591 |     } catch (err: any) {
 592 |       return { error: err?.message || 'Failed to sync new item' };
 593 |     }
 594 |   },

 648 |     if (!client) return {};
 649 | 
 650 |     try {
 651 |       const { error } = await client
 652 |         .from('pools')
 653 |         .update({ status })
 654 |         .match({ id: poolId });

 662 |   async syncItemClaimsList(
 663 |     poolId: string,
 664 |     itemId: string,
 665 |     claims: ParticipantShare[]
 666 |   ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
 667 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 668 |     if (!client) return {};
 669 | 
 670 |     try {
 671 |       await client.from('item_claims').delete().match({ item_id: itemId });
 672 |       if (claims.length > 0) {
 673 |         const rows = claims.map((c) => ({
 674 |           pool_id: poolId,
 675 |           item_id: itemId,
 676 |           user_id: c.userId,
 677 |           user_name: c.userName,
 678 |           avatar_color: c.avatarColor,
 679 |           quantity: c.quantity,
 680 |           updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
 681 |         }));
 682 |         const { error } = await client
 683 |           .from('item_claims')
 684 |           .upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'item_id,user_id' });
 685 |         if (error) throw error;
 686 |       }
 687 |       return {};
 688 |     } catch (err: any) {
 689 |       return { error: err?.message || 'Failed to sync item claims list' };
 690 |     }
 691 |   },
 692 | 
 693 |   subscribeToRealtime(onRemoteChange: (eventTable: string) => void): () => void {
 694 |     const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
 695 |     if (!client) {
 696 |       return () => {};
 697 |     }
 698 | 
 699 |     if (activeChannel) {
 700 |       activeChannel.unsubscribe();
 701 |       activeChannel = null;
 702 |     }
 703 | 
 704 |     const channel = client
 705 |       .channel('poolcart-live-sync')
 706 |       .on(
 707 |         'postgres_changes',
 708 |         { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'item_claims' },
 709 |         () => onRemoteChange('item_claims')
 710 |       )
 711 |       .on(
 712 |         'postgres_changes',
 713 |         { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'catalog_items' },
 714 |         () => onRemoteChange('catalog_items')
 715 |       )
 716 |       .on(
 717 |         'postgres_changes',
 718 |         { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'pool_participants' },
 719 |         () => onRemoteChange('pool_participants')
 720 |       )
 721 |       .on(
 722 |         'postgres_changes',
 723 |         { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'pools' },
 724 |         () => onRemoteChange('pools')
 725 |       )
 726 |       .on(
 727 |         'postgres_changes',
 728 |         { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'pool_messages' },
 729 |         () => onRemoteChange('pool_messages')
 730 |       )
 731 |       .subscribe();
 732 | 
 733 |     activeChannel = channel;
 734 | 
 735 |     return () => {
 736 |       channel.unsubscribe();
 737 |       if (activeChannel === channel) {
 738 |         activeChannel = null;
 739 |       }
 740 |     };
 741 |   },
 742 | 
 743 |   async resetDemoData(): Promise<GroupBuyPool[]> {
 744 |     await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY_POOLS, JSON.stringify(INITIAL_POOLS));
 745 |     if (currentConfig.enabled) {
 746 |       await this.seedSupabasePools(INITIAL_POOLS);
 747 |     }
 748 |     return INITIAL_POOLS;
 749 |   },
 750 | };

===== FILE: src/utils/calculations.ts =====
   1 | import { CatalogItem, GroupBuyPool, UserCostBreakdown } from '../types/groupBuy';
   2 | 
   3 | export interface CaseSplitStatus {
   4 |   totalClaimedUnits: number;
   5 |   caseSize: number;
   6 |   fullCasesCount: number;
   7 |   committedUnits: number; // Units that are part of full cases
   8 |   remainderUnits: number; // Units waiting to fill the next case
   9 |   neededForNextCase: number;
  10 |   isFullyMatched: boolean;
  11 | }
  12 | 
  13 | export interface WholesaleManifestLine {
  14 |   itemId: string;
  15 |   itemName: string;
  16 |   emoji: string;
  17 |   mode: 'individual' | 'case_split';
  18 |   orderQuantityLabel: string; // e.g. "4 units (12-ct bag)" or "1 full case (6 units)"
  19 |   committedUnits: number;
  20 |   unfilledRemainderUnits: number;
  21 |   neededToFillNextCase: number;
  22 |   unitPrice: number;
  23 |   lineTotal: number;
  24 | }
  25 | 
  26 | /**
  27 |  * Calculates how many full cases are formed for a `case_split` item,
  28 |  * and how many units are still needed to complete the current partial case.
  29 |  */
  30 | export function getCaseSplitStatus(item: CatalogItem): CaseSplitStatus {
  31 |   const totalClaimedUnits = item.claims.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.quantity, 0);
  32 |   if (item.mode !== 'case_split' || !item.caseSize || item.caseSize <= 1) {
  33 |     return {
  34 |       totalClaimedUnits,
  35 |       caseSize: 1,
  36 |       fullCasesCount: totalClaimedUnits,
  37 |       committedUnits: totalClaimedUnits,
  38 |       remainderUnits: 0,
  39 |       neededForNextCase: 0,
  40 |       isFullyMatched: totalClaimedUnits > 0,
  41 |     };
  42 |   }
  43 | 
  44 |   const caseSize = item.caseSize;
  45 |   const fullCasesCount = Math.floor(totalClaimedUnits / caseSize);
  46 |   const committedUnits = fullCasesCount * caseSize;
  47 |   const remainderUnits = totalClaimedUnits % caseSize;
  48 |   const neededForNextCase = remainderUnits === 0 ? 0 : caseSize - remainderUnits;
  49 | 
  50 |   return {
  51 |     totalClaimedUnits,
  52 |     caseSize,
  53 |     fullCasesCount,
  54 |     committedUnits,
  55 |     remainderUnits,
  56 |     neededForNextCase,
  57 |     isFullyMatched: totalClaimedUnits > 0 && remainderUnits === 0,
  58 |   };
  59 | }
  60 | 
  61 | /**
  62 |  * Calculates pool-wide wholesale progress:
  63 |  * - committedPoolTotal: subtotal of all individual items + completed cases
  64 |  * - pendingCaseTotal: subtotal of partial cases waiting for more neighbors
  65 |  * - delivery fee status (waived once freeDeliveryThreshold is reached, otherwise split evenly among active buyers)
  66 |  */
  67 | export function getPoolProgressSummary(pool: GroupBuyPool) {
  68 |   let committedPoolTotal = 0;
  69 |   let pendingCaseTotal = 0;
  70 |   let totalRetailValue = 0;
  71 | 
  72 |   pool.items.forEach((item) => {
  73 |     const status = getCaseSplitStatus(item);
  74 |     committedPoolTotal += status.committedUnits * item.unitPrice;
  75 |     pendingCaseTotal += status.remainderUnits * item.unitPrice;
  76 |     totalRetailValue += status.totalClaimedUnits * item.retailPrice;
  77 |   });
  78 | 
  79 |   const combinedPoolTotal = committedPoolTotal + pendingCaseTotal;
  80 |   const minimumProgressRatio = Math.min(1, combinedPoolTotal / pool.minimumOrderAmount);
  81 |   const freeDeliveryProgressRatio = Math.min(1, combinedPoolTotal / pool.freeDeliveryThreshold);
  82 | 
  83 |   const amountNeededForMinimum = Math.max(0, pool.minimumOrderAmount - combinedPoolTotal);
  84 |   const amountNeededForFreeDelivery = Math.max(0, pool.freeDeliveryThreshold - combinedPoolTotal);
  85 | 
  86 |   const isMinimumMet = combinedPoolTotal >= pool.minimumOrderAmount;
  87 |   const isFreeDeliveryUnlocked = combinedPoolTotal >= pool.freeDeliveryThreshold;
  88 | 
  89 |   // Count participants who have claimed at least 1 unit across any item
  90 |   const activeParticipantIds = new Set<string>();
  91 |   pool.items.forEach((item) => {
  92 |     item.claims.forEach((c) => {
  93 |       if (c.quantity > 0) activeParticipantIds.add(c.userId);
  94 |     });
  95 |   });
  96 | 
  97 |   const activeBuyerCount = Math.max(1, activeParticipantIds.size);
  98 |   const currentDeliveryFeePerPerson = isFreeDeliveryUnlocked
  99 |     ? 0
 100 |     : pool.deliveryFee / activeBuyerCount;
 101 | 
 102 |   const totalPoolSavings = Math.max(0, totalRetailValue - combinedPoolTotal);
 103 | 
 104 |   return {
 105 |     committedPoolTotal,
 106 |     pendingCaseTotal,
 107 |     combinedPoolTotal,
 108 |     minimumProgressRatio,
 109 |     freeDeliveryProgressRatio,
 110 |     amountNeededForMinimum,
 111 |     amountNeededForFreeDelivery,
 112 |     isMinimumMet,
 113 |     isFreeDeliveryUnlocked,
 114 |     activeBuyerCount,
 115 |     currentDeliveryFeePerPerson,
 116 |     totalPoolSavings,
 117 |   };
 118 | }
 119 | 
 120 | /**
 121 |  * Generates per-participant itemized ledger and totals for checkout & host tracking.
 122 |  */
 123 | export function getParticipantBreakdowns(pool: GroupBuyPool): UserCostBreakdown[] {
 124 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
 125 | 
 126 |   return pool.participants.map((participant) => {
 127 |     let committedSubtotal = 0;
 128 |     let pendingCaseSubtotal = 0;
 129 |     let totalEstimatedSavings = 0;
 130 |     const lineItems: UserCostBreakdown['lineItems'] = [];
 131 | 
 132 |     pool.items.forEach((item) => {
 133 |       const claim = item.claims.find((c) => c.userId === participant.id);
 134 |       if (!claim || claim.quantity <= 0) return;
 135 | 
 136 |       const status = getCaseSplitStatus(item);
 137 |       const itemSubtotal = claim.quantity * item.unitPrice;
 138 |       const itemSavings = claim.quantity * Math.max(0, item.retailPrice - item.unitPrice);
 139 |       totalEstimatedSavings += itemSavings;
 140 | 
 141 |       const isCaseSplit = item.mode === 'case_split';
 142 |       const caseFilled = !isCaseSplit || status.remainderUnits === 0;
 143 | 
 144 |       if (caseFilled) {
 145 |         committedSubtotal += itemSubtotal;
 146 |       } else {
 147 |         pendingCaseSubtotal += itemSubtotal;
 148 |       }
 149 | 
 150 |       lineItems.push({
 151 |         itemId: item.id,
 152 |         itemName: item.name,
 153 |         emoji: item.emoji,
 154 |         quantity: claim.quantity,
 155 |         unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
 156 |         subtotal: itemSubtotal,
 157 |         isCaseSplit,
 158 |         caseFilled,
 159 |         caseProgressText: isCaseSplit
 160 |           ? `${status.totalClaimedUnits}/${status.caseSize} case units claimed`
 161 |           : undefined,
 162 |       });
 163 |     });
 164 | 
 165 |     const hasItems = lineItems.length > 0;
 166 |     const estimatedDeliveryShare = hasItems ? summary.currentDeliveryFeePerPerson : 0;
 167 |     const totalDue = committedSubtotal + pendingCaseSubtotal + estimatedDeliveryShare;
 168 | 
 169 |     return {
 170 |       userId: participant.id,
 171 |       userName: participant.name,
 172 |       avatarColor: participant.avatarColor,
 173 |       committedSubtotal,
 174 |       pendingCaseSubtotal,
 175 |       estimatedDeliveryShare,
 176 |       totalEstimatedSavings,
 177 |       totalDue,
 178 |       paidStatus: participant.paidStatus,
 179 |       pickedUp: participant.pickedUp,
 180 |       lineItems,
 181 |     };
 182 |   });
 183 | }
 184 | 
 185 | /**
 186 |  * Generates the Host Wholesale Supplier Purchase Order Manifest
 187 |  */
 188 | export function getWholesaleOrderManifest(pool: GroupBuyPool): {
 189 |   lines: WholesaleManifestLine[];
 190 |   partialCaseItems: WholesaleManifestLine[];
 191 |   totalSupplierMerchandiseCost: number;
 192 |   supplierDeliveryFee: number;
 193 |   grandTotalSupplierInvoice: number;
 194 | } {
 195 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
 196 |   const lines: WholesaleManifestLine[] = [];
 197 |   const partialCaseItems: WholesaleManifestLine[] = [];
 198 | 
 199 |   pool.items.forEach((item) => {
 200 |     const status = getCaseSplitStatus(item);
 201 |     if (status.totalClaimedUnits === 0) return;
 202 | 
 203 |     const lineTotal = status.committedUnits * item.unitPrice;
 204 |     const orderQuantityLabel =
 205 |       item.mode === 'case_split'
 206 |         ? `${status.fullCasesCount} full case${status.fullCasesCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} (${status.committedUnits} × ${item.unitLabel})`
 207 |         : `${status.committedUnits} × ${item.unitLabel}`;
 208 | 
 209 |     const entry: WholesaleManifestLine = {
 210 |       itemId: item.id,
 211 |       itemName: item.name,
 212 |       emoji: item.emoji,
 213 |       mode: item.mode,
 214 |       orderQuantityLabel,
 215 |       committedUnits: status.committedUnits,
 216 |       unfilledRemainderUnits: status.remainderUnits,
 217 |       neededToFillNextCase: status.neededForNextCase,
 218 |       unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
 219 |       lineTotal,
 220 |     };
 221 | 
 222 |     lines.push(entry);
 223 |     if (item.mode === 'case_split' && status.remainderUnits > 0) {
 224 |       partialCaseItems.push(entry);
 225 |     }
 226 |   });
 227 | 
 228 |   const totalSupplierMerchandiseCost = lines.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.lineTotal, 0);
 229 |   const supplierDeliveryFee = summary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked ? 0 : pool.deliveryFee;
 230 |   const grandTotalSupplierInvoice = totalSupplierMerchandiseCost + supplierDeliveryFee;
 231 | 
 232 |   return {
 233 |     lines,
 234 |     partialCaseItems,
 235 |     totalSupplierMerchandiseCost,
 236 |     supplierDeliveryFee,
 237 |     grandTotalSupplierInvoice,
 238 |   };
 239 | }

 241 | /**
 242 |  * Generates formatted text for native iOS/Android sharing (invite neighbors or export supplier manifest)
 243 |  */
 244 | export function buildShareablePoolInviteText(pool: GroupBuyPool): string {
 245 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
 246 |   const partialCases = pool.items
 247 |     .map((item) => ({ item, status: getCaseSplitStatus(item) }))
 248 |     .filter((x) => x.item.mode === 'case_split' && x.status.neededForNextCase > 0);
 249 | 
 250 |   const partialLines =
 251 |     partialCases.length > 0
 252 |       ? `\n📦 Open Bulk Case Splits needing shares:\n` +
 253 |         partialCases
 254 |           .map(
 255 |             (x) =>
 256 |               `• ${x.item.emoji} ${x.item.name}: ${x.status.neededForNextCase} more needed ($${x.item.unitPrice.toFixed(2)}/${x.item.unitLabel})`
 257 |           )
 258 |           .join('\n')
 259 |       : '';
 260 | 
 261 |   return (
 262 |     `🥬 Join our neighborhood grocery group buy on PoolCart!\n` +
 263 |     `Pool: ${pool.title} (${pool.supplierName})\n` +
 264 |     `Host: ${pool.hostName} • Pickup: ${pool.pickupWindow} at ${pool.pickupAddress}\n` +
 265 |     `Progress: $${summary.combinedPoolTotal.toFixed(2)} / $${pool.minimumOrderAmount} minimum (${
 266 |       summary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked
 267 |         ? 'FREE wholesale delivery unlocked!'
 268 |         : `$${summary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery.toFixed(2)} away from free delivery`
 269 |     })\n` +
 270 |     `Cutoff: ${pool.cutoffTime}` +
 271 |     partialLines
 272 |   );
 273 | }
 274 | 
 275 | export function buildSupplierOrderExportText(pool: GroupBuyPool): string {
 276 |   const manifest = getWholesaleOrderManifest(pool);
 277 |   const breakdowns = getParticipantBreakdowns(pool).filter((b) => b.lineItems.length > 0);
 278 | 
 279 |   const itemLines = manifest.lines
 280 |     .filter((l) => l.committedUnits > 0)
 281 |     .map(
 282 |       (l) =>
 283 |         `• ${l.emoji} ${l.itemName}: ${l.orderQuantityLabel} — $${l.lineTotal.toFixed(2)}`
 284 |     )
 285 |     .join('\n');
 286 | 
 287 |   const neighborLines = breakdowns
 288 |     .map(
 289 |       (b) =>
 290 |         `• ${b.userName} (${b.paidStatus === 'paid' ? 'PAID' : 'PENDING'}): $${b.totalDue.toFixed(2)} — ` +
 291 |         b.lineItems.map((li) => `${li.emoji}×${li.quantity}`).join(', ')
 292 |     )
 293 |     .join('\n');
 294 | 
 295 |   return (
 296 |     `📋 WHOLESALE PURCHASE ORDER — ${pool.title}\n` +
 297 |     `Supplier: ${pool.supplierName}\n` +
 298 |     `Host: ${pool.hostName} | Pickup: ${pool.pickupWindow}\n\n` +
 299 |     `--- SUPPLIER MASTER ORDER ---\n` +
 300 |     `${itemLines || '(No committed full units yet)'}\n` +
 301 |     `Merchandise Subtotal: $${manifest.totalSupplierMerchandiseCost.toFixed(2)}\n` +
 302 |     `Wholesale Delivery Fee: $${manifest.supplierDeliveryFee.toFixed(2)}\n` +
 303 |     `Total Supplier Invoice: $${manifest.grandTotalSupplierInvoice.toFixed(2)}\n\n` +
 304 |     `--- NEIGHBOR PICKUP & SPLIT CHECKLIST ---\n` +
 305 |     `${neighborLines}`
 306 |   );
 307 | }

  29 | export const AddItemModal: React.FC<AddItemModalProps> = ({
  30 |   visible,
  31 |   activeUser,
  32 |   onClose,
  33 |   onAddItem,
  34 | }) => {
  35 |   const [name, setName] = useState('');
  36 |   const [emoji, setEmoji] = useState('📦');
  37 |   const [unitLabel, setUnitLabel] = useState('1 unit');
  38 |   const [unitPrice, setUnitPrice] = useState('8.50');
  39 |   const [retailPrice, setRetailPrice] = useState('12.99');
  40 |   const [mode, setMode] = useState<ItemMode>('case_split');
  41 |   const [caseSize, setCaseSize] = useState('6');
  42 | 
  43 |   const handleSave = () => {
  44 |     if (!name.trim()) return;
  45 |     const parsedUnit = parseFloat(unitPrice) || 5;
  46 |     const parsedRetail = parseFloat(retailPrice) || parsedUnit * 1.35;
  47 |     const parsedCase = parseInt(caseSize, 10) || 4;
  48 | 
  49 |     const item: CatalogItem = {
  50 |       id: `item_${Date.now()}`,
  51 |       name: name.trim(),
  52 |       category: 'Neighborhood Addition',
  53 |       emoji: emoji.trim() || '🛒',
  54 |       mode,
  55 |       unitLabel: unitLabel.trim() || '1 share',
  56 |       unitPrice: parsedUnit,
  57 |       retailPrice: parsedRetail,
  58 |       caseSize: mode === 'case_split' ? parsedCase : undefined,
  59 |       claims: [
  60 |         {
  61 |           userId: activeUser.id,
  62 |           userName: activeUser.name,
  63 |           avatarColor: activeUser.avatarColor,
  64 |           quantity: 1,
  65 |         },
  66 |       ],
  67 |     };
  68 | 
  69 |     onAddItem(item);
  70 |     setName('');
  71 |     onClose();
  72 |   };
  73 | 
  74 |   return (
  75 |     <Modal visible={visible} animationType="slide" transparent>
  76 |       <KeyboardAvoidingView
  77 |         behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
  78 |         style={styles.overlay}
  79 |       >
  80 |         <View style={styles.sheet}>
  81 |           <Text style={styles.title}>Add Item or Bulk Case Split</Text>
  82 |           <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
  83 |             Pitching as {activeUser.name} • First share claimed automatically.
  84 |           </Text>
  85 | 
  86 |           <ScrollView style={styles.formScroll}>
  87 |             <View style={styles.modeSwitchRow}>
  88 |               <TouchableOpacity
  89 |                 style={[
  90 |                   styles.modeOption,
  91 |                   mode === 'case_split' && styles.modeOptionActive,
  92 |                 ]}
  93 |                 onPress={() => setMode('case_split')}
  94 |               >
  95 |                 <Text
  96 |                   style={[
  97 |                     styles.modeOptionText,
  98 |                     mode === 'case_split' && styles.modeOptionTextActive,
  99 |                   ]}
 100 |                 >
 101 |                   📦 Split Bulk Case
 102 |                 </Text>
 103 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 104 |               <TouchableOpacity
 105 |                 style={[
 106 |                   styles.modeOption,

 113 |                     styles.modeOptionText,
 114 |                     mode === 'individual' && styles.modeOptionTextActive,
 115 |                   ]}
 116 |                 >
 117 |                   🛒 Individual Wholesale

 128 |             />
 129 |

 131 |               <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 8 }}>
 132 |                 <Text style={styles.label}>Emoji</Text>
 133 |                 <TextInput
 134 |                   style={styles.input}
 135 |                   value={emoji}
 136 |                   onChangeText={setEmoji}
 137 |                 />
 138 |               </View>
 139 |               <View style={{ flex: 2 }}>
 140 |                 <Text style={styles.label}>Unit / Share Label</Text>

 143 |                   placeholder="e.g. 2 bottles (1/6 case)"
 144 |                   value={unitLabel}
 145 |                   onChangeText={setUnitLabel}
 146 |                 />
 147 |               </View>
 148 |             </View>
 149 | 
 150 |             <View style={styles.row}>
 151 |               <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 8 }}>
 152 |                 <Text style={styles.label}>Wholesale Price ($)</Text>
 153 |                 <TextInput
 154 |                   style={styles.input}
 155 |                   keyboardType="decimal-pad"
 156 |                   value={unitPrice}
 157 |                   onChangeText={setUnitPrice}
 158 |                 />
 159 |               </View>
 160 |               <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
