 457 | interface JoinByCodeModalProps {
 458 |   visible: boolean;
 459 |   pools: GroupBuyPool[];
 460 |   onClose: () => void;
 461 |   onSelectPoolId: (poolId: string) => void;
 462 | }
 463 | 
 464 | export const JoinByCodeModal: React.FC<JoinByCodeModalProps> = ({
 465 |   visible,
 466 |   pools,
 467 |   onClose,
 468 |   onSelectPoolId,
 469 | }) => {
 470 |   const [codeInput, setCodeInput] = useState('');
 471 |   const [errorText, setErrorText] = useState('');
 472 | 
 473 |   const handleJoin = () => {
 474 |     const cleaned = codeInput.trim().toUpperCase();
 475 |     if (!cleaned) return;
 476 | 
 477 |     const matched = pools.find(
 478 |       (p) => p.joinCode.toUpperCase() === cleaned || p.id.toUpperCase() === cleaned
 479 |     );
 480 | 
 481 |     if (!matched) {
 482 |       setErrorText(
 483 |         `No neighborhood pool found with code "${cleaned}". Try one of the active codes below!`
 484 |       );
 485 |       return;
 486 |     }
 487 | 
 488 |     setErrorText('');
 489 |     setCodeInput('');
 490 |     onSelectPoolId(matched.id);
 491 |     onClose();
 492 |   };
 493 | 
 494 |   return (
 495 |     <Modal visible={visible} animationType="fade" transparent>
 496 |       <KeyboardAvoidingView
 497 |         behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
 498 |         style={styles.overlay}
 499 |       >
 500 |         <View style={styles.sheet}>
 501 |           <Text style={styles.title}>🔑 Join a Pool by Invite Code</Text>
 502 |           <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
 503 |             Enter the 6-character code shared by your neighborhood host (or tap an active pool code below).
 504 |           </Text>
 505 | 
 506 |           <TextInput
 507 |             style={[styles.input, styles.codeInputBig]}
 508 |             placeholder="e.g. VG-FARM or WEEE-88"
 509 |             autoCapitalize="characters"
 510 |             autoCorrect={false}
 511 |             value={codeInput}
 512 |             onChangeText={(val) => {
 513 |               setCodeInput(val);
 514 |               setErrorText('');
 515 |             }}
 516 |           />
 517 | 
 518 |           {errorText ? <Text style={styles.errorMsg}>{errorText}</Text> : null}
 519 | 
 520 |           <Text style={styles.label}>Active Neighborhood Codes Near You</Text>
 521 |           <View style={styles.activeCodesWrap}>
 522 |             {pools.map((p) => (
 523 |               <TouchableOpacity
 524 |                 key={p.id}
 525 |                 style={styles.activeCodeChip}
 526 |                 onPress={() => {
 527 |                   onSelectPoolId(p.id);
 528 |                   onClose();
 529 |                 }}
 530 |               >
 531 |                 <Text style={styles.activeCodeBadge}>{p.joinCode}</Text>
 532 |                 <Text style={styles.activeCodeTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
 533 |                   {p.title}
 534 |                 </Text>
 535 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 536 |             ))}
 537 |           </View>
 538 | 
 539 |           <View style={[styles.actionsRow, { marginTop: 16 }]}>
 540 |             <TouchableOpacity style={styles.cancelBtn} onPress={onClose}>
 541 |               <Text style={styles.cancelBtnText}>Close</Text>
 542 |             </TouchableOpacity>
 543 |             <TouchableOpacity style={styles.saveBtn} onPress={handleJoin}>
 544 |               <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>Join Pool</Text>
 545 |             </TouchableOpacity>
 546 |           </View>
 547 |         </View>
 548 |       </KeyboardAvoidingView>
 549 |     </Modal>
 550 |   );
 551 | };

   1 | import React from 'react';
   2 | import { View, Text, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
   3 | import { CatalogItem } from '../types/groupBuy';
   4 | import { getCaseSplitStatus } from '../utils/calculations';
   5 | 
   6 | interface Props {
   7 |   item: CatalogItem;
   8 |   currentUserId: string;
   9 |   isPoolLocked?: boolean;
  10 |   onUpdateQuantity: (itemId: string, delta: number) => void;
  11 | }
  12 | 
  13 | export const CatalogItemCard: React.FC<Props> = ({
  14 |   item,
  15 |   currentUserId,
  16 |   isPoolLocked = false,
  17 |   onUpdateQuantity,
  18 | }) => {
  19 |   const userClaim = item.claims.find((c) => c.userId === currentUserId);
  20 |   const myQty = userClaim ? userClaim.quantity : 0;
  21 |   const caseStatus = getCaseSplitStatus(item);
  22 |   const savingsPct = Math.round(
  23 |     ((item.retailPrice - item.unitPrice) / item.retailPrice) * 100
  24 |   );
  25 | 
  26 |   // Build slot indicators for case_split mode
  27 |   const renderCaseSlots = () => {
  28 |     if (item.mode !== 'case_split' || !item.caseSize) return null;
  29 | 
  30 |     // Show current active case slots (or completed case if exact multiple)
  31 |     const slotsInCurrentView =
  32 |       caseStatus.remainderUnits === 0 && caseStatus.totalClaimedUnits > 0
  33 |         ? item.caseSize
  34 |         : caseStatus.remainderUnits;
  35 | 
  36 |     const slots = Array.from({ length: item.caseSize }, (_, idx) => {
  37 |       const filled = idx < slotsInCurrentView;
  38 |       return (
  39 |         <View
  40 |           key={idx}
  41 |           style={[
  42 |             styles.caseSlot,
  43 |             filled ? styles.caseSlotFilled : styles.caseSlotEmpty,
  44 |           ]}
  45 |         />
  46 |       );
  47 |     });
  48 | 
  49 |     return (
  50 |       <View style={styles.caseSplitContainer}>
  51 |         <View style={styles.caseHeaderRow}>
  52 |           <Text style={styles.caseTitle}>
  53 |             📦 Bulk Case Split ({item.caseSize} units/case)
  54 |           </Text>
  55 |           <Text
  56 |             style={[
  57 |               styles.caseStatusBadge,
  58 |               caseStatus.neededForNextCase === 0 && caseStatus.totalClaimedUnits > 0
  59 |                 ? styles.caseMatchedText
  60 |                 : styles.casePendingText,
  61 |             ]}
  62 |           >
  63 |             {caseStatus.totalClaimedUnits === 0
  64 |               ? `0/${item.caseSize} claimed`
  65 |               : caseStatus.neededForNextCase === 0
  66 |               ? `✓ ${caseStatus.fullCasesCount} full case(s) locked!`
  67 |               : `${caseStatus.neededForNextCase} more needed to fill case`}
  68 |           </Text>
  69 |         </View>
  70 |         <View style={styles.slotsRow}>{slots}</View>
  71 |       </View>
  72 |     );
  73 |   };
  74 | 
  75 |   return (
  76 |     <View style={styles.card}>
  77 |       <View style={styles.mainRow}>
  78 |         <View style={styles.emojiContainer}>
  79 |           <Text style={styles.emoji}>{item.emoji}</Text>
  80 |         </View>
  81 | 
  82 |         <View style={styles.infoCol}>
  83 |           <View style={styles.tagRow}>
  84 |             <View
  85 |               style={[
  86 |                 styles.modeBadge,
  87 |                 item.mode === 'case_split' ? styles.badgeCase : styles.badgeIndiv,
  88 |               ]}
  89 |             >
  90 |               <Text
  91 |                 style={[
  92 |                   styles.modeBadgeText,
  93 |                   item.mode === 'case_split'
  94 |                     ? styles.badgeCaseText
  95 |                     : styles.badgeIndivText,
  96 |                 ]}
  97 |               >
  98 |                 {item.mode === 'case_split' ? 'SPLIT CASE' : 'DIRECT WHOLESALE'}
  99 |               </Text>
 100 |             </View>
 101 |             {savingsPct > 0 && (
 102 |               <View style={styles.saveBadge}>
 103 |                 <Text style={styles.saveBadgeText}>Save {savingsPct}%</Text>
 104 |               </View>
 105 |             )}
 106 |           </View>
 107 | 
 108 |           <Text style={styles.itemName}>{item.name}</Text>
 109 |           <Text style={styles.unitLabel}>{item.unitLabel}</Text>
 110 | 
 111 |           <View style={styles.priceRow}>
 112 |             <Text style={styles.unitPrice}>${item.unitPrice.toFixed(2)}</Text>
 113 |             <Text style={styles.retailPrice}>${item.retailPrice.toFixed(2)} retail</Text>
 114 |           </View>
 115 |         </View>
 116 | 
 117 |         {/* Stepper Control */}
 118 |         <View style={styles.stepperCol}>
 119 |           {isPoolLocked ? (
 120 |             <View style={styles.lockedBadge}>
 121 |               <Text style={styles.lockedBadgeText}>
 122 |                 🔒 {myQty > 0 ? `Locked (${myQty})` : 'Pool Locked'}
 123 |               </Text>
 124 |             </View>
 125 |           ) : myQty === 0 ? (
 126 |             <TouchableOpacity
 127 |               style={styles.joinButton}
 128 |               onPress={() => onUpdateQuantity(item.id, 1)}
 129 |               activeOpacity={0.8}
 130 |             >
 131 |               <Text style={styles.joinButtonText}>
 132 |                 {item.mode === 'case_split' ? '+ Claim Share' : '+ Add'}
 133 |               </Text>
 134 |             </TouchableOpacity>
 135 |           ) : (
 136 |             <View style={styles.stepperBox}>
 137 |               <TouchableOpacity
 138 |                 style={styles.stepBtn}
 139 |                 onPress={() => onUpdateQuantity(item.id, -1)}
 140 |               >
 141 |                 <Text style={styles.stepBtnText}>−</Text>
 142 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 143 |               <Text style={styles.qtyText}>{myQty}</Text>
 144 |               <TouchableOpacity
 145 |                 style={styles.stepBtn}
 146 |                 onPress={() => onUpdateQuantity(item.id, 1)}
 147 |               >
 148 |                 <Text style={styles.stepBtnText}>+</Text>
 149 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 150 |             </View>
 151 |           )}
 152 |           {myQty > 0 && (
 153 |             <Text style={styles.mySubtotalText}>
 154 |               You: ${(myQty * item.unitPrice).toFixed(2)}
 155 |             </Text>
 156 |           )}
 157 |         </View>
 158 |       </View>
 159 | 
 160 |       {/* Case Split Bar if applicable */}
 161 |       {renderCaseSlots()}
 162 | 
 163 |       {/* Neighbors claiming this item */}
 164 |       {item.claims.length > 0 && (
 165 |         <View style={styles.claimsRow}>
 166 |           <Text style={styles.claimsLabel}>Pool claims: </Text>
 167 |           <View style={styles.pillsWrap}>
 168 |             {item.claims.map((c) => (
 169 |               <View
 170 |                 key={c.userId}
 171 |                 style={[
 172 |                   styles.claimPill,
 173 |                   c.userId === currentUserId && styles.myClaimPill,
 174 |                 ]}
 175 |               >
 176 |                 <View
 177 |                   style={[styles.dot, { backgroundColor: c.avatarColor }]}
 178 |                 />
 179 |                 <Text style={styles.claimPillText}>
 180 |                   {c.userName.split(' ')[0]} ×{c.quantity}
 181 |                 </Text>
 182 |               </View>
 183 |             ))}
 184 |           </View>
 185 |         </View>
 186 |       )}
 187 |     </View>
 188 |   );
 189 | };

===== FILE: src/components/HostOrderSheetView.tsx =====
   1 | import React from 'react';
   2 | import {
   3 |   View,
   4 |   Text,
   5 |   StyleSheet,
   6 |   TouchableOpacity,
   7 |   Share,
   8 | } from 'react-native';
   9 | import { GroupBuyPool } from '../types/groupBuy';
  10 | import {
  11 |   buildSupplierOrderExportText,
  12 |   getParticipantBreakdowns,
  13 |   getPoolProgressSummary,
  14 |   getWholesaleOrderManifest,
  15 | } from '../utils/calculations';
  16 | 
  17 | interface Props {
  18 |   pool: GroupBuyPool;
  19 |   onToggleLockPool: () => void;
  20 |   onRerunWeeklyPool: () => void;
  21 |   onHostCoverRemainder: (itemId: string, neededQty: number) => void;
  22 |   onTrimPartialCase: (itemId: string) => void;
  23 |   onTogglePaidStatus: (userId: string) => void;
  24 |   onTogglePickedUp: (userId: string) => void;
  25 | }
  26 | 
  27 | export const HostOrderSheetView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  28 |   pool,
  29 |   onToggleLockPool,
  30 |   onRerunWeeklyPool,
  31 |   onHostCoverRemainder,
  32 |   onTrimPartialCase,
  33 |   onTogglePaidStatus,
  34 |   onTogglePickedUp,
  35 | }) => {
  36 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
  37 |   const manifest = getWholesaleOrderManifest(pool);
  38 |   const breakdowns = getParticipantBreakdowns(pool).filter(
  39 |     (b) => b.lineItems.length > 0
  40 |   );
  41 | 
  42 |   const isLocked = pool.status === 'locked';
  43 | 
  44 |   const handleExportManifest = async () => {
  45 |     try {
  46 |       const text = buildSupplierOrderExportText(pool);
  47 |       await Share.share({
  48 |         title: `${pool.title} — Wholesale Order Manifest`,
  49 |         message: text,
  50 |       });
  51 |     } catch (err) {
  52 |       console.warn('Share error:', err);
  53 |     }
  54 |   };
  55 | 
  56 |   return (
  57 |     <View style={styles.container}>
  58 |       {/* Host Cutoff & Lock Control Card */}
  59 |       <View
  60 |         style={[
  61 |           styles.controlCard,
  62 |           isLocked ? styles.controlCardLocked : styles.controlCardOpen,
  63 |         ]}
  64 |       >
  65 |         <View style={styles.controlHeaderRow}>
  66 |           <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 10 }}>
  67 |             <Text style={styles.controlBadge}>
  68 |               {isLocked
  69 |                 ? '🔒 POOL LOCKED FOR WHOLESALE ORDER'
  70 |                 : '🟢 POOL OPEN FOR NEIGHBOR CLAIMS'}
  71 |             </Text>
  72 |             <Text style={styles.controlTitle}>
  73 |               {isLocked
  74 |                 ? 'Cutoff Reached — Ready to Order & Distribute'
  75 |                 : `Order Cutoff: ${pool.cutoffTime}`}
  76 |             </Text>
  77 |             <Text style={styles.controlSub}>
  78 |               {isLocked
  79 |                 ? 'Claims are frozen so you can place the supplier order and check off neighbor pickups.'
  80 |                 : summary.isMinimumMet
  81 |                 ? 'Wholesale minimum is met! Lock the pool when you are ready to finalize the order.'
  82 |                 : `Pool is $${summary.amountNeededForMinimum.toFixed(2)} away from the $${pool.minimumOrderAmount} supplier minimum.`}
  83 |             </Text>
  84 |           </View>
  85 | 
  86 |           <TouchableOpacity
  87 |             style={[
  88 |               styles.lockButton,
  89 |               isLocked ? styles.unlockButton : styles.lockNowButton,
  90 |             ]}
  91 |             onPress={onToggleLockPool}
  92 |           >
  93 |             <Text style={styles.lockButtonText}>
  94 |               {isLocked ? '🔓 Reopen Pool' : '🔒 Lock Pool'}
  95 |             </Text>
  96 |           </TouchableOpacity>
  97 |         </View>
  98 | 
  99 |         <View style={styles.rerunRow}>
 100 |           <Text style={styles.rerunHint}>
 101 |             Want to run this exact supplier catalog again next week?
 102 |           </Text>
 103 |           <TouchableOpacity
 104 |             style={styles.rerunBtn}
 105 |             onPress={onRerunWeeklyPool}
 106 |           >
 107 |             <Text style={styles.rerunBtnText}>🔁 Rerun Next Week</Text>
 108 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 109 |         </View>
 110 |       </View>
 111 | 
 112 |       {/* Partial Bulk Cases Resolution Alert */}
 113 |       {manifest.partialCaseItems.length > 0 && (
 114 |         <View style={styles.partialAlertCard}>
 115 |           <Text style={styles.partialAlertTitle}>
 116 |             ⚠️ Unfilled Bulk Cases Need Resolution ({manifest.partialCaseItems.length})
 117 |           </Text>
 118 |           <Text style={styles.partialAlertSub}>
 119 |             Wholesale suppliers only ship full cases. Before placing the supplier
 120 |             order, either claim the remaining units as Host or trim the partial case:
 121 |           </Text>
 122 | 
 123 |           {manifest.partialCaseItems.map((pItem) => (
 124 |             <View key={pItem.itemId} style={styles.partialItemRow}>
 125 |               <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
 126 |                 <Text style={styles.partialItemName}>
 127 |                   {pItem.emoji} {pItem.itemName}
 128 |                 </Text>
 129 |                 <Text style={styles.partialItemMeta}>
 130 |                   {pItem.unfilledRemainderUnits} unit(s) waiting • Need{' '}
 131 |                   <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>
 132 |                     {pItem.neededToFillNextCase} more
 133 |                   </Text>{' '}
 134 |                   (${(pItem.neededToFillNextCase * pItem.unitPrice).toFixed(2)})
 135 |                   to complete case
 136 |                 </Text>
 137 |               </View>
 138 | 
 139 |               <View style={styles.partialActionBtns}>
 140 |                 <TouchableOpacity
 141 |                   style={styles.coverBtn}
 142 |                   onPress={() =>
 143 |                     onHostCoverRemainder(
 144 |                       pItem.itemId,
 145 |                       pItem.neededToFillNextCase
 146 |                     )
 147 |                   }
 148 |                 >
 149 |                   <Text style={styles.coverBtnText}>
 150 |                     + Cover {pItem.neededToFillNextCase}
 151 |                   </Text>
 152 |                 </TouchableOpacity>
 153 | 
 154 |                 <TouchableOpacity
 155 |                   style={styles.trimBtn}
 156 |                   onPress={() => onTrimPartialCase(pItem.itemId)}
 157 |                 >
 158 |                   <Text style={styles.trimBtnText}>Drop Partial</Text>
 159 |                 </TouchableOpacity>
 160 |               </View>
 161 |             </View>
 162 |           ))}
 163 |         </View>
 164 |       )}
 165 | 
 166 |       {/* Master Wholesale Purchase Order Manifest */}
 167 |       <View style={styles.manifestCard}>
 168 |         <View style={styles.manifestHeaderRow}>
 169 |           <View>
 170 |             <Text style={styles.sectionLabel}>SUPPLIER PURCHASE ORDER</Text>
 171 |             <Text style={styles.manifestSupplier}>{pool.supplierName}</Text>
 172 |           </View>
 173 |           <TouchableOpacity
 174 |             style={styles.exportButton}
 175 |             onPress={handleExportManifest}
 176 |           >
 177 |             <Text style={styles.exportButtonText}>📤 Share Order Sheet</Text>
 178 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 179 |         </View>
 180 | 
 181 |         {manifest.lines.filter((l) => l.committedUnits > 0).length === 0 ? (
 182 |           <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
 183 |             No full wholesale items or completed cases yet.
 184 |           </Text>
 185 |         ) : (
 186 |           manifest.lines
 187 |             .filter((l) => l.committedUnits > 0)
 188 |             .map((line) => (
 189 |               <View key={line.itemId} style={styles.manifestLine}>
 190 |                 <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
 191 |                   <Text style={styles.manifestItemName}>
 192 |                     {line.emoji} {line.itemName}
 193 |                   </Text>
 194 |                   <Text style={styles.manifestQtySub}>
 195 |                     Order qty: {line.orderQuantityLabel}
 196 |                   </Text>
 197 |                 </View>
 198 |                 <Text style={styles.manifestLineTotal}>
 199 |                   ${line.lineTotal.toFixed(2)}
 200 |                 </Text>
 201 |               </View>
 202 |             ))
 203 |         )}
 204 | 
 205 |         <View style={styles.invoiceSummary}>
 206 |           <View style={styles.invoiceRow}>
 207 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceLabel}>Wholesale Merchandise</Text>
 208 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceValue}>
 209 |               ${manifest.totalSupplierMerchandiseCost.toFixed(2)}
 210 |             </Text>
 211 |           </View>
 212 |           <View style={styles.invoiceRow}>
 213 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceLabel}>Supplier Delivery Fee</Text>
 214 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceValue}>
 215 |               {manifest.supplierDeliveryFee === 0
 216 |                 ? 'FREE (Waived)'
 217 |                 : `$${manifest.supplierDeliveryFee.toFixed(2)}`}
 218 |             </Text>
 219 |           </View>
 220 |           <View style={[styles.invoiceRow, styles.invoiceGrandRow]}>
 221 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceGrandLabel}>
 222 |               Est. Supplier Invoice Total
 223 |             </Text>
 224 |             <Text style={styles.invoiceGrandValue}>
 225 |               ${manifest.grandTotalSupplierInvoice.toFixed(2)}
 226 |             </Text>
 227 |           </View>
 228 |         </View>
 229 |       </View>
 230 | 
 231 |       {/* Porch Pickup & Distribution Checklist */}
 232 |       <View style={styles.manifestCard}>
 233 |         <Text style={styles.sectionLabel}>
 234 |           PORCH PICKUP & NEIGHBOR DISTRIBUTION CHECKLIST
 235 |         </Text>
 236 |         <Text style={styles.pickupSub}>
 237 |           {pool.pickupAddress} • {pool.pickupWindow}
 238 |         </Text>
 239 | 
 240 |         {breakdowns.map((b) => (
 241 |           <View key={b.userId} style={styles.pickupMemberRow}>
 242 |             <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 8 }}>
 243 |               <View style={styles.pickupNameWrap}>
 244 |                 <View
 245 |                   style={[styles.dot, { backgroundColor: b.avatarColor }]}
 246 |                 />
 247 |                 <Text style={styles.pickupMemberName}>{b.userName}</Text>
 248 |                 <Text style={styles.pickupMemberTotal}>
 249 |                   • ${b.totalDue.toFixed(2)}
 250 |                 </Text>
 251 |               </View>
 252 |               <Text style={styles.pickupItemsSummary}>
 253 |                 {b.lineItems
 254 |                   .map((li) => `${li.emoji} ${li.itemName} ×${li.quantity}`)
 255 |                   .join('  |  ')}
 256 |               </Text>
 257 |             </View>
 258 | 
 259 |             <View style={styles.pickupChipsCol}>
 260 |               <TouchableOpacity
 261 |                 style={[
 262 |                   styles.smallStatusChip,
 263 |                   b.paidStatus === 'paid'
 264 |                     ? styles.chipGreen
 265 |                     : styles.chipAmber,
 266 |                 ]}
 267 |                 onPress={() => onTogglePaidStatus(b.userId)}
 268 |               >
 269 |                 <Text
 270 |                   style={[
 271 |                     styles.smallChipText,
 272 |                     b.paidStatus === 'paid'
 273 |                       ? styles.chipTextGreen
 274 |                       : styles.chipTextAmber,
 275 |                   ]}
 276 |                 >
 277 |                   {b.paidStatus === 'paid' ? '✓ Paid' : 'Unpaid'}
 278 |                 </Text>
 279 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 280 | 
 281 |               <TouchableOpacity
 282 |                 style={[
 283 |                   styles.smallStatusChip,
 284 |                   b.pickedUp ? styles.chipTeal : styles.chipGray,
 285 |                 ]}
 286 |                 onPress={() => onTogglePickedUp(b.userId)}
 287 |               >
 288 |                 <Text
 289 |                   style={[
 290 |                     styles.smallChipText,
 291 |                     b.pickedUp ? styles.chipTextTeal : styles.chipTextGray,
 292 |                   ]}
 293 |                 >
 294 |                   {b.pickedUp ? '🛍️ Picked Up' : 'Mark Picked Up'}
 295 |                 </Text>
 296 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 297 |             </View>
 298 |           </View>
 299 |         ))}
 300 |       </View>
 301 |     </View>
 302 |   );
 303 | };

===== FILE: src/components/PoolChatView.tsx =====
   1 | import React, { useState } from 'react';
   2 | import {
   3 |   View,
   4 |   Text,
   5 |   TextInput,
   6 |   TouchableOpacity,
   7 |   StyleSheet,
   8 | } from 'react-native';
   9 | import { GroupBuyPool, Participant, PoolMessage } from '../types/groupBuy';
  10 | import { getCaseSplitStatus } from '../utils/calculations';
  11 | 
  12 | interface Props {
  13 |   pool: GroupBuyPool;
  14 |   activeUser: Participant;
  15 |   onSendMessage: (text: string, isAnnouncement: boolean) => void;
  16 | }
  17 | 
  18 | export const PoolChatView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  19 |   pool,
  20 |   activeUser,
  21 |   onSendMessage,
  22 | }) => {
  23 |   const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
  24 |   const [postAsAnnouncement, setPostAsAnnouncement] = useState(false);
  25 | 
  26 |   const messages: PoolMessage[] = pool.messages || [];
  27 |   const pinnedAnnouncements = messages.filter((m) => m.isAnnouncement);
  28 | 
  29 |   // Quick helper prompts so neighbors or hosts can post 1-tap case-split calls
  30 |   const openCaseSplits = pool.items
  31 |     .map((item) => ({ item, status: getCaseSplitStatus(item) }))
  32 |     .filter(
  33 |       (x) => x.item.mode === 'case_split' && x.status.neededForNextCase > 0
  34 |     );
  35 | 
  36 |   const handleSend = () => {
  37 |     if (!draft.trim()) return;
  38 |     onSendMessage(draft.trim(), postAsAnnouncement);
  39 |     setDraft('');
  40 |     setPostAsAnnouncement(false);
  41 |   };
  42 | 
  43 |   const handleQuickPrompt = (promptText: string, asAnnouncement = false) => {
  44 |     onSendMessage(promptText, asAnnouncement);
  45 |   };
  46 | 
  47 |   return (
  48 |     <View style={styles.container}>
  49 |       {/* Pool Invite Code & Pickup Info Banner */}
  50 |       <View style={styles.codeCard}>
  51 |         <View>
  52 |           <Text style={styles.codeLabel}>NEIGHBORHOOD INVITE CODE</Text>
  53 |           <Text style={styles.codeValue}>🔑 {pool.joinCode}</Text>
  54 |         </View>
  55 |         <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end' }}>
  56 |           <Text style={styles.codeHost}>Host: {pool.hostName}</Text>
  57 |           <Text style={styles.codePickup}>{pool.pickupWindow}</Text>
  58 |         </View>
  59 |       </View>
  60 | 
  61 |       {/* Pinned Host Announcements */}
  62 |       {pinnedAnnouncements.length > 0 && (
  63 |         <View style={styles.pinnedBox}>
  64 |           <Text style={styles.pinnedHeader}>
  65 |             📢 PINNED HOST ANNOUNCEMENT
  66 |           </Text>
  67 |           {pinnedAnnouncements.slice(-2).map((ann) => (
  68 |             <View key={ann.id} style={styles.pinnedItem}>
  69 |               <Text style={styles.pinnedText}>{ann.text}</Text>
  70 |               <Text style={styles.pinnedMeta}>
  71 |                 — {ann.userName} • {ann.createdAt}
  72 |               </Text>
  73 |             </View>
  74 |           ))}
  75 |         </View>
  76 |       )}
  77 | 
  78 |       {/* 1-Tap Quick Prompts for Case Splits & Porch Drop Updates */}
  79 |       <View style={styles.quickPromptsWrap}>
  80 |         <Text style={styles.quickPromptsLabel}>
  81 |           ⚡ 1-TAP NEIGHBOR & HOST UPDATES
  82 |         </Text>
  83 |         <View style={styles.quickChipsRow}>
  84 |           {openCaseSplits.slice(0, 2).map(({ item, status }) => (
  85 |             <TouchableOpacity
  86 |               key={item.id}
  87 |               style={styles.quickChip}
  88 |               onPress={() =>
  89 |                 handleQuickPrompt(
  90 |                   `Who wants to split ${item.emoji} ${item.name}? Only ${status.neededForNextCase} more share(s) needed ($${item.unitPrice.toFixed(2)} each) to lock the case!`
  91 |                 )
  92 |               }
  93 |             >
  94 |               <Text style={styles.quickChipText}>
  95 |                 📣 Rally {item.emoji} ({status.neededForNextCase} left)
  96 |               </Text>
  97 |             </TouchableOpacity>
  98 |           ))}
  99 | 
 100 |           <TouchableOpacity
 101 |             style={[styles.quickChip, styles.quickChipHost]}
 102 |             onPress={() =>
 103 |               handleQuickPrompt(
 104 |                 `📦 Wholesale delivery has arrived at ${pool.pickupAddress}! Cooler is out on the porch for pickup (${pool.pickupWindow}).`,
 105 |                 true
 106 |               )
 107 |             }
 108 |           >
 109 |             <Text style={styles.quickChipHostText}>
 110 |               📢 Post "Order Arrived!"
 111 |             </Text>
 112 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 113 |         </View>
 114 |       </View>
 115 | 
 116 |       {/* Message Feed */}
 117 |       <Text style={styles.feedHeader}>
 118 |         POOL DISCUSSION & Q&A ({messages.length})
 119 |       </Text>
 120 | 
 121 |       {messages.length === 0 ? (
 122 |         <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
 123 |           <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
 124 |             No messages yet. Ask a question about an item or rally neighbors to
 125 |             split a bulk case!
 126 |           </Text>
 127 |         </View>
 128 |       ) : (
 129 |         messages.map((msg) => {
 130 |           const isMe = msg.userId === activeUser.id;
 131 |           return (
 132 |             <View
 133 |               key={msg.id}
 134 |               style={[
 135 |                 styles.msgCard,
 136 |                 isMe && styles.msgCardMe,
 137 |                 msg.isAnnouncement && styles.msgCardAnnouncement,
 138 |               ]}
 139 |             >
 140 |               <View style={styles.msgTopRow}>
 141 |                 <View style={styles.authorRow}>
 142 |                   <View
 143 |                     style={[
 144 |                       styles.avatar,
 145 |                       { backgroundColor: msg.avatarColor },
 146 |                     ]}
 147 |                   >
 148 |                     <Text style={styles.avatarText}>
 149 |                       {msg.userName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
 150 |                     </Text>
 151 |                   </View>
 152 |                   <Text style={styles.authorName}>{msg.userName}</Text>
 153 |                   {msg.isAnnouncement && (
 154 |                     <View style={styles.annBadge}>
 155 |                       <Text style={styles.annBadgeText}>ANNOUNCEMENT</Text>
 156 |                     </View>
 157 |                   )}
 158 |                 </View>
 159 |                 <Text style={styles.msgTime}>{msg.createdAt}</Text>
 160 |               </View>
 161 |               <Text style={styles.msgBody}>{msg.text}</Text>
 162 |             </View>
 163 |           );
 164 |         })
 165 |       )}
 166 | 
 167 |       {/* Composer */}
 168 |       <View style={styles.composerCard}>
 169 |         <View style={styles.composerTopRow}>
 170 |           <Text style={styles.composerIdentity}>
 171 |             Posting as <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>{activeUser.name}</Text>
 172 |           </Text>
 173 |           <TouchableOpacity
 174 |             style={[
 175 |               styles.annToggle,
 176 |               postAsAnnouncement && styles.annToggleActive,
 177 |             ]}
 178 |             onPress={() => setPostAsAnnouncement(!postAsAnnouncement)}
 179 |           >
 180 |             <Text
 181 |               style={[
 182 |                 styles.annToggleText,
 183 |                 postAsAnnouncement && styles.annToggleTextActive,
 184 |               ]}
 185 |             >
 186 |               {postAsAnnouncement ? '📢 Pin as Announcement' : 'Pin Announcement'}
 187 |             </Text>
 188 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 189 |         </View>
 190 | 
 191 |         <View style={styles.inputRow}>
 192 |           <TextInput
 193 |             style={styles.input}
 194 |             placeholder="Ask the host or rally neighbors for a case split..."
 195 |             value={draft}
 196 |             onChangeText={setDraft}
 197 |             multiline
 198 |           />
 199 |           <TouchableOpacity
 200 |             style={[
 201 |               styles.sendBtn,
 202 |               !draft.trim() && styles.sendBtnDisabled,
 203 |             ]}
 204 |             onPress={handleSend}
 205 |             disabled={!draft.trim()}
 206 |           >
 207 |             <Text style={styles.sendBtnText}>Send</Text>
 208 |           </TouchableOpacity>
 209 |         </View>
 210 |       </View>
 211 |     </View>
 212 |   );
 213 | };

===== FILE: src/components/PoolProgressCard.tsx =====
   1 | import React from 'react';
   2 | import { View, Text, StyleSheet } from 'react-native';
   3 | import { GroupBuyPool } from '../types/groupBuy';
   4 | import { getPoolProgressSummary } from '../utils/calculations';
   5 | 
   6 | interface Props {
   7 |   pool: GroupBuyPool;
   8 | }
   9 | 
  10 | export const PoolProgressCard: React.FC<Props> = ({ pool }) => {
  11 |   const summary = getPoolProgressSummary(pool);
  12 |   const minPercent = Math.round(summary.minimumProgressRatio * 100);
  13 |   const freeDelivPercent = Math.round(summary.freeDeliveryProgressRatio * 100);
  14 | 
  15 |   return (
  16 |     <View style={styles.card}>
  17 |       <View style={styles.topRow}>
  18 |         <View>
  19 |           <Text style={styles.label}>POOL WHOLESALE TOTAL</Text>
  20 |           <View style={styles.amountRow}>
  21 |             <Text style={styles.currentAmount}>${summary.combinedPoolTotal.toFixed(2)}</Text>
  22 |             <Text style={styles.targetAmount}> / ${pool.minimumOrderAmount} min</Text>
  23 |           </View>
  24 |         </View>
  25 |         <View
  26 |           style={[
  27 |             styles.statusBadge,
  28 |             summary.isMinimumMet ? styles.statusSuccess : styles.statusPending,
  29 |           ]}
  30 |         >
  31 |           <Text
  32 |             style={[
  33 |               styles.statusText,
  34 |               summary.isMinimumMet ? styles.statusTextSuccess : styles.statusTextPending,
  35 |             ]}
  36 |           >
  37 |             {summary.isMinimumMet
  38 |               ? '✓ Minimum Met!'
  39 |               : `$${summary.amountNeededForMinimum.toFixed(2)} to unlock`}
  40 |           </Text>
  41 |         </View>
  42 |       </View>
  43 | 
  44 |       {/* Wholesale Minimum Progress Bar */}
  45 |       <View style={styles.progressTrack}>
  46 |         <View
  47 |           style={[
  48 |             styles.progressFill,
  49 |             { width: `${minPercent}%` },
  50 |             summary.isMinimumMet ? styles.fillGreen : styles.fillTeal,
  51 |           ]}
  52 |         />
  53 |       </View>
  54 | 
  55 |       {/* Free Delivery Tier Progress */}
  56 |       <View style={styles.tierRow}>
  57 |         <Text style={styles.tierLabel}>
  58 |           {summary.isFreeDeliveryUnlocked
  59 |             ? `🎉 Free wholesale delivery unlocked! ($${pool.freeDeliveryThreshold}+ reached)`
  60 |             : `🚚 Add $${summary.amountNeededForFreeDelivery.toFixed(2)} more to waive the $${pool.deliveryFee} delivery fee (${freeDelivPercent}%)`}
  61 |         </Text>
  62 |       </View>
  63 | 
  64 |       {/* Quick Stats */}
  65 |       <View style={styles.statsFooter}>
  66 |         <View style={styles.statBox}>
  67 |           <Text style={styles.statValue}>{summary.activeBuyerCount} Neighbors</Text>

===== FILE: src/components/SplitLedgerView.tsx =====
   1 | import React from 'react';
   2 | import {
   3 |   View,
   4 |   Text,
   5 |   StyleSheet,
   6 |   TouchableOpacity,
   7 |   Linking,
   8 |   Alert,
   9 | } from 'react-native';
  10 | import { GroupBuyPool } from '../types/groupBuy';
  11 | import { getParticipantBreakdowns } from '../utils/calculations';
  12 | 
  13 | interface Props {
  14 |   pool: GroupBuyPool;
  15 |   currentUserId: string;
  16 |   onTogglePaidStatus: (userId: string) => void;
  17 | }
  18 | 
  19 | export const SplitLedgerView: React.FC<Props> = ({
  20 |   pool,
  21 |   currentUserId,
  22 |   onTogglePaidStatus,
  23 | }) => {
  24 |   const breakdowns = getParticipantBreakdowns(pool);
  25 |   const venmoHandle = pool.hostVenmoHandle || 'PoolCart-Host';
  26 | 
  27 |   const handlePayWithVenmo = async (
  28 |     userId: string,
  29 |     amount: number,
  30 |     userName: string
  31 |   ) => {
  32 |     const note = encodeURIComponent(
  33 |       `PoolCart: ${pool.title} (${userName.split(' ')[0]})`
  34 |     );
  35 |     const amountStr = amount.toFixed(2);
  36 |     const venmoDeepLink = `venmo://paycharge?txn=pay&recipients=${encodeURIComponent(
  37 |       venmoHandle
  38 |     )}&amount=${amountStr}&note=${note}`;
  39 |     const venmoWebFallback = `https://venmo.com/${encodeURIComponent(
  40 |       venmoHandle
  41 |     )}?txn=pay&amount=${amountStr}&note=${note}`;
  42 | 
  43 |     try {
  44 |       const supported = await Linking.canOpenURL(venmoDeepLink);
  45 |       if (supported) {
  46 |         await Linking.openURL(venmoDeepLink);
  47 |       } else {
  48 |         await Linking.openURL(venmoWebFallback);
  49 |       }
  50 |       // Also offer to mark as paid
  51 |       if (pool.participants.find((p) => p.id === userId)?.paidStatus !== 'paid') {
  52 |         onTogglePaidStatus(userId);
  53 |       }
  54 |     } catch {
  55 |       Alert.alert(
  56 |         'Venmo Payment Details',
  57 |         `Send $${amountStr} to @${venmoHandle} with note "${pool.title}".`
  58 |       );
  59 |     }
  60 |   };
  61 | 
  62 |   return (
  63 |     <View style={styles.container}>
  64 |       <View style={styles.pickupBanner}>
  65 |         <Text style={styles.pickupTitle}>📍 Pickup & Drop-off Logistics</Text>
  66 |         <Text style={styles.pickupDetail}>
  67 |           Host: {pool.hostName} (@{venmoHandle})
  68 |         </Text>
  69 |         <Text style={styles.pickupDetail}>Address: {pool.pickupAddress}</Text>
  70 |         <Text style={styles.pickupDetail}>Window: {pool.pickupWindow}</Text>
  71 |         <Text style={styles.cutoffNote}>
  72 |           {pool.status === 'locked'
  73 |             ? '🔒 Pool Locked — Ready for Settlement & Pickup'
  74 |             : `Order Cutoff: ${pool.cutoffTime}`}
  75 |         </Text>
  76 |       </View>
  77 | 
  78 |       <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>
  79 |         NEIGHBOR COST SPLIT & SETTLEMENT LEDGER
  80 |       </Text>
  81 | 
  82 |       {breakdowns.map((b) => {
  83 |         const isMe = b.userId === currentUserId;
  84 |         return (
  85 |           <View
  86 |             key={b.userId}
  87 |             style={[styles.memberCard, isMe && styles.myMemberCard]}
  88 |           >
  89 |             <View style={styles.memberHeader}>
  90 |               <View style={styles.memberNameRow}>
  91 |                 <View
  92 |                   style={[styles.avatar, { backgroundColor: b.avatarColor }]}
  93 |                 >
  94 |                   <Text style={styles.avatarInitial}>
  95 |                     {b.userName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
  96 |                   </Text>
  97 |                 </View>
  98 |                 <View>
  99 |                   <Text style={styles.memberName}>{b.userName}</Text>
 100 |                   <Text style={styles.savingsSub}>
 101 |                     Saving ${b.totalEstimatedSavings.toFixed(2)} vs. retail
 102 |                   </Text>
 103 |                 </View>
 104 |               </View>
 105 | 
 106 |               <TouchableOpacity
 107 |                 style={[
 108 |                   styles.paidChip,
 109 |                   b.paidStatus === 'paid'
 110 |                     ? styles.paidChipGreen
 111 |                     : styles.paidChipAmber,
 112 |                 ]}
 113 |                 onPress={() => onTogglePaidStatus(b.userId)}
 114 |               >
 115 |                 <Text
 116 |                   style={[
 117 |                     styles.paidChipText,
 118 |                     b.paidStatus === 'paid'
 119 |                       ? styles.paidTextGreen
 120 |                       : styles.paidTextAmber,
 121 |                   ]}
 122 |                 >
 123 |                   {b.paidStatus === 'paid' ? '✓ Paid' : 'Mark Paid'}
 124 |                 </Text>
 125 |               </TouchableOpacity>
 126 |             </View>
 127 | 
 128 |             {/* Line Items */}
 129 |             {b.lineItems.length === 0 ? (
 130 |               <Text style={styles.emptyItemsText}>
 131 |                 No items claimed in this pool yet.
 132 |               </Text>
 133 |             ) : (
 134 |               <View style={styles.itemsList}>
 135 |                 {b.lineItems.map((li) => (
 136 |                   <View key={li.itemId} style={styles.lineItemRow}>
 137 |                     <View style={styles.lineItemLeft}>
 138 |                       <Text style={styles.lineItemTitle}>
 139 |                         {li.emoji} {li.itemName} (×{li.quantity})
 140 |                       </Text>
 141 |                       {li.isCaseSplit && (
 142 |                         <Text
 143 |                           style={[
 144 |                             styles.caseSubNote,
 145 |                             li.caseFilled
 146 |                               ? { color: '#15803D' }
 147 |                               : { color: '#9333EA' },
 148 |                           ]}
 149 |                         >
 150 |                           {li.caseFilled
 151 |                             ? '✓ Case filled'
 152 |                             : `⏳ Waiting for case to fill (${li.caseProgressText})`}
 153 |                         </Text>
 154 |                       )}
 155 |                     </View>
 156 |                     <Text style={styles.lineItemPrice}>
 157 |                       ${li.subtotal.toFixed(2)}
 158 |                     </Text>
 159 |                   </View>
 160 |                 ))}
 161 | 
 162 |                 <View style={styles.feeRow}>
 163 |                   <Text style={styles.feeLabel}>Shared Wholesale Delivery</Text>
 164 |                   <Text style={styles.feeValue}>
 165 |                     {b.estimatedDeliveryShare === 0
 166 |                       ? 'FREE (Waived)'
 167 |                       : `$${b.estimatedDeliveryShare.toFixed(2)}`}
 168 |                   </Text>
 169 |                 </View>
 170 | 
 171 |                 <View style={styles.totalRow}>
 172 |                   <Text style={styles.totalLabel}>Total Pool Share</Text>
 173 |                   <Text style={styles.totalValue}>
 174 |                     ${b.totalDue.toFixed(2)}
 175 |                   </Text>
 176 |                 </View>
 177 | 
 178 |                 {/* 1-Tap Venmo Deep Link Settlement Button */}
 179 |                 {b.paidStatus !== 'paid' && b.totalDue > 0 && (
 180 |                   <TouchableOpacity
 181 |                     style={styles.venmoButton}
 182 |                     onPress={() =>
 183 |                       handlePayWithVenmo(b.userId, b.totalDue, b.userName)
 184 |                     }
 185 |                   >
 186 |                     <Text style={styles.venmoButtonText}>
 187 |                       💸 Pay ${b.totalDue.toFixed(2)} to @{venmoHandle} via Venmo
 188 |                     </Text>
 189 |                   </TouchableOpacity>
 190 |                 )}
 191 |               </View>
 192 |             )}
 193 |           </View>
 194 |         );
 195 |       })}
 196 |     </View>
 197 |   );
 198 | };

===== FILE: src/components/SupabaseSetupCard.tsx =====
   1 | import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
   2 | import {
   3 |   View,
   4 |   Text,
   5 |   TextInput,
   6 |   TouchableOpacity,
   7 |   StyleSheet,
   8 |   ActivityIndicator,
   9 | } from 'react-native';
  10 | import { Participant } from '../types/groupBuy';
  11 | import {
  12 |   DEMO_PROFILES,
  13 |   PoolRepository,
  14 |   SupabaseConfig,
  15 | } from '../services/repository';
  16 | 
  17 | interface Props {
  18 |   activeUser: Participant;
  19 |   onSelectUser: (user: Participant) => void;
  20 |   syncStatus: {
  21 |     mode: 'local' | 'supabase_live' | 'supabase_error';
  22 |     lastSyncedAt: string | null;
  23 |     errorMessage?: string;
  24 |     lastRealtimeEvent?: string | null;
  25 |   };
  26 |   onConfigSaved: (config: SupabaseConfig) => Promise<void>;
  27 |   onSimulateNeighborAction: () => void;
  28 |   onResetDemo: () => void;
  29 | }
  30 | 
  31 | export const SupabaseSetupCard: React.FC<Props> = ({
  32 |   activeUser,
  33 |   onSelectUser,
  34 |   syncStatus,
  35 |   onConfigSaved,
  36 |   onSimulateNeighborAction,
  37 |   onResetDemo,
  38 | }) => {
  39 |   const existingConfig = PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig();
  40 |   const [url, setUrl] = useState(existingConfig.url);
  41 |   const [anonKey, setAnonKey] = useState(existingConfig.anonKey);
  42 |   const [customName, setCustomName] = useState('');
  43 |   const [customNeighborhood, setCustomNeighborhood] = useState('Victoria Gardens');
  44 |   const [isConnecting, setIsConnecting] = useState(false);
  45 | 
  46 |   useEffect(() => {
  47 |     const cfg = PoolRepository.getCurrentConfig();
  48 |     setUrl(cfg.url);
  49 |     setAnonKey(cfg.anonKey);
  50 |   }, [syncStatus.mode]);
  51 | 
  52 |   const handleConnectSupabase = async () => {
  53 |     if (!url.trim() || !anonKey.trim()) return;
  54 |     setIsConnecting(true);
  55 |     try {
  56 |       await onConfigSaved({
  57 |         url: url.trim(),
  58 |         anonKey: anonKey.trim(),
  59 |         enabled: true,
  60 |       });
  61 |     } finally {
  62 |       setIsConnecting(false);
  63 |     }
  64 |   };
  65 | 
  66 |   const handleDisconnectSupabase = async () => {
  67 |     setIsConnecting(true);
  68 |     try {
  69 |       await onConfigSaved({
  70 |         url: url.trim(),
  71 |         anonKey: anonKey.trim(),
  72 |         enabled: false,
  73 |       });
  74 |     } finally {
  75 |       setIsConnecting(false);
  76 |     }
  77 |   };
  78 | 
  79 |   const handleCreateCustomProfile = () => {
  80 |     if (!customName.trim()) return;
  81 |     const colors = ['#0D9488', '#E11D48', '#2563EB', '#7C3AED', '#D97706', '#059669'];
  82 |     const randomColor = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];
  83 |     const newProfile: Participant = {
  84 |       id: `u_${customName.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '_')}_${Date.now().toString().slice(-3)}`,
  85 |       name: customName.trim(),
  86 |       neighborhood: customNeighborhood.trim() || 'Rancho Cucamonga',
  87 |       avatarColor: randomColor,
  88 |       paidStatus: 'pending',
  89 |     };
  90 |     onSelectUser(newProfile);
  91 |     setCustomName('');
  92 |   };
  93 | 
  94 |   return (
  95 |     <View style={styles.container}>
  96 |       {/* Live Status Banner */}
  97 |       <View
  98 |         style={[
  99 |           styles.statusBanner,
 100 |           syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live'
 101 |             ? styles.bannerLive
 102 |             : syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_error'
 103 |             ? styles.bannerError
 104 |             : styles.bannerLocal,
 105 |         ]}
 106 |       >
 107 |         <View style={styles.statusRow}>
 108 |           <View
 109 |             style={[
 110 |               styles.statusDot,
 111 |               syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live'
 112 |                 ? styles.dotLive
 113 |                 : syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_error'
 114 |                 ? styles.dotError
 115 |                 : styles.dotLocal,
 116 |             ]}
 117 |           />
 118 |           <Text style={styles.statusTitle}>
 119 |             {syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_live'
 120 |               ? 'Supabase Realtime Connected (Live Multi-Neighbor Sync)'
 121 |               : syncStatus.mode === 'supabase_error'
 122 |               ? 'Supabase Connection Error (Using Local Fallback)'
 123 |               : 'Local Device Mode (Ready for Supabase Cloud Sync)'}
 124 |           </Text>
 125 |         </View>
 126 | 
 127 |         {syncStatus.lastSyncedAt && (
 128 |           <Text style={styles.statusMeta}>
 129 |             Last synced: {syncStatus.lastSyncedAt}
 130 |             {syncStatus.lastRealtimeEvent
 131 |               ? ` • Live event: ${syncStatus.lastRealtimeEvent}`
 132 |               : ''}
 133 |           </Text>
 134 |         )}
 135 | 
 136 |         {syncStatus.errorMessage && (
 137 |           <Text style={styles.errorText}>{syncStatus.errorMessage}</Text>
 138 |         )}
 139 |       </View>
 140 | 
 141 |       {/* Multi-Neighbor Identity Switcher */}
 142 |       <View style={styles.card}>
 143 |         <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>
 144 |           👤 Active Neighbor Identity (Test Multi-User Claims)
 145 |         </Text>
 146 |         <Text style={styles.cardSub}>
 147 |           Switch between neighbor profiles on this device—or open the app on two
 148 |           phones—to test how claims combine toward wholesale minimums and case splits.
 149 |         </Text>
 150 | 
 151 |         <View style={styles.profileGrid}>
 152 |           {DEMO_PROFILES.map((prof) => {
 153 |             const isSelected = prof.id === activeUser.id;
 154 |             return (
 155 |               <TouchableOpacity
 156 |                 key={prof.id}
 157 |                 style={[
 158 |                   styles.profilePill,
 159 |                   isSelected && styles.profilePillActive,
 160 |                 ]}
 161 |                 onPress={() => onSelectUser(prof)}
 162 |               >
 163 |                 <View
 164 |                   style={[
 165 |                     styles.avatarDot,
 166 |                     { backgroundColor: prof.avatarColor },
 167 |                   ]}
 168 |                 />
 169 |                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
 170 |                   <Text
 171 |                     style={[
 172 |                       styles.profileName,
 173 |                       isSelected && styles.profileNameActive,
 174 |                     ]}
 175 |                     numberOfLines={1}
 176 |                   >
 177 |                     {prof.name}
 178 |                   </Text>
 179 |                   <Text style={styles.profileHood} numberOfLines={1}>
 180 |                     {prof.neighborhood}

===== FILE: supabase/schema.sql =====
   1 | -- ============================================================================
   2 | -- PoolCart — Supabase PostgreSQL + Realtime + Atomic RPC Schema
   3 | -- Paste this into your Supabase SQL Editor (https://supabase.com/dashboard)
   4 | -- and click "Run" to create the tables, policies, Realtime channels, and
   5 | -- the race-condition-safe `claim_item_share` stored procedure.
   6 | -- ============================================================================
   7 | 
   8 | -- 1. Pools Table
   9 | create table if not exists public.pools (
  10 |   id text primary key,
  11 |   join_code text not null default 'POOL-01',
  12 |   title text not null,
  13 |   supplier_name text not null,
  14 |   supplier_badge text not null,
  15 |   category text not null,
  16 |   host_name text not null,
  17 |   host_venmo_handle text,
  18 |   neighborhood text not null,
  19 |   pickup_address text not null,
  20 |   pickup_window text not null,
  21 |   cutoff_time text not null,
  22 |   minimum_order_amount numeric not null default 200,
  23 |   delivery_fee numeric not null default 20,
  24 |   free_delivery_threshold numeric not null default 300,
  25 |   status text not null default 'open',
  26 |   created_at timestamptz not null default now()
  27 | );
  28 | 
  29 | -- 2. Pool Participants Table
  30 | create table if not exists public.pool_participants (
  31 |   pool_id text not null references public.pools(id) on delete cascade,
  32 |   user_id text not null,
  33 |   user_name text not null,
  34 |   neighborhood text not null default 'Victoria Gardens',
  35 |   avatar_color text not null default '#0D9488',
  36 |   is_host boolean not null default false,
  37 |   paid_status text not null default 'pending' check (paid_status in ('pending', 'paid')),
  38 |   picked_up boolean not null default false,
  39 |   updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  40 |   primary key (pool_id, user_id)
  41 | );
  42 | 
  43 | -- 3. Catalog Items Table
  44 | create table if not exists public.catalog_items (
  45 |   id text primary key,
  46 |   pool_id text not null references public.pools(id) on delete cascade,
  47 |   name text not null,
  48 |   category text not null,
  49 |   emoji text not null default '🛒',
  50 |   mode text not null check (mode in ('individual', 'case_split')),
  51 |   unit_label text not null,
  52 |   unit_price numeric not null,
  53 |   retail_price numeric not null,
  54 |   case_size integer,
  55 |   max_cases integer,
  56 |   created_at timestamptz not null default now()
  57 | );
  58 | 
  59 | -- 4. Item Claims Table
  60 | create table if not exists public.item_claims (
  61 |   pool_id text not null references public.pools(id) on delete cascade,
  62 |   item_id text not null references public.catalog_items(id) on delete cascade,
  63 |   user_id text not null,
  64 |   user_name text not null,
  65 |   avatar_color text not null,
  66 |   quantity integer not null check (quantity >= 0),
  67 |   updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  68 |   primary key (item_id, user_id)
  69 | );
  70 | 
  71 | -- 5. Pool Messages & Host Announcements Table
  72 | create table if not exists public.pool_messages (
  73 |   id text primary key,
  74 |   pool_id text not null references public.pools(id) on delete cascade,
  75 |   user_id text not null,
  76 |   user_name text not null,
  77 |   avatar_color text not null default '#0D9488',
  78 |   is_host boolean not null default false,
  79 |   is_announcement boolean not null default false,
  80 |   text text not null,
  81 |   created_at_label text not null,
  82 |   created_at timestamptz not null default now()
  83 | );
  84 | 
  85 | -- 6. Atomic Case-Split Claim RPC Function (`claim_item_share`)
  86 | -- Uses `FOR UPDATE` row-level locking on `catalog_items` and `pools` so if two
  87 | -- neighbors tap the last share of a bulk case at the exact same millisecond:
  88 | --   • It serializes their claims cleanly without lost updates
  89 | --   • Rejects mutations if the pool was locked by the host
  90 | --   • Enforces `max_cases` cap if the host capped the number of bulk cases
  91 | --   • Returns exact case completion & rollover metadata to trigger notifications
  92 | create or replace function public.claim_item_share(
  93 |   p_pool_id text,
  94 |   p_item_id text,
  95 |   p_user_id text,
  96 |   p_user_name text,
  97 |   p_avatar_color text,
  98 |   p_neighborhood text,
  99 |   p_delta integer
 100 | )
 101 | returns jsonb
 102 | language plpgsql
 103 | security definer
 104 | as $$
 105 | declare
 106 |   v_pool_status text;
 107 |   v_item_mode text;
 108 |   v_case_size integer;
 109 |   v_max_cases integer;
 110 |   v_current_user_qty integer := 0;
 111 |   v_other_users_qty integer := 0;
 112 |   v_prev_total integer := 0;
 113 |   v_next_user_qty integer := 0;
 114 |   v_next_total integer := 0;
 115 |   v_max_allowed_units integer;
 116 |   v_capped boolean := false;
 117 |   v_prev_full_cases integer := 0;
 118 |   v_next_full_cases integer := 0;
 119 |   v_remainder integer := 0;
 120 | begin
 121 |   -- Lock pool row to verify cutoff lock status
 122 |   select status into v_pool_status
 123 |   from public.pools
 124 |   where id = p_pool_id
 125 |   for update;
 126 | 
 127 |   if v_pool_status = 'locked' then
 128 |     raise exception 'Pool is locked for wholesale ordering; claims cannot be modified.';
 129 |   end if;
 130 | 
 131 |   -- Lock catalog_item row to serialize concurrent case-split claims
 132 |   select mode, coalesce(case_size, 1), max_cases
 133 |   into v_item_mode, v_case_size, v_max_cases
 134 |   from public.catalog_items
 135 |   where id = p_item_id and pool_id = p_pool_id
 136 |   for update;
 137 | 
 138 |   if not found then
 139 |     raise exception 'Catalog item not found in pool.';
 140 |   end if;
 141 | 
 142 |   -- Ensure participant exists in pool_participants
 143 |   insert into public.pool_participants (pool_id, user_id, user_name, neighborhood, avatar_color)
 144 |   values (p_pool_id, p_user_id, p_user_name, coalesce(p_neighborhood, 'Victoria Gardens'), p_avatar_color)
 145 |   on conflict (pool_id, user_id) do update
 146 |   set user_name = excluded.user_name,
 147 |       avatar_color = excluded.avatar_color,
 148 |       updated_at = now();
 149 | 
 150 |   -- Compute current quantities under lock
 151 |   select coalesce(quantity, 0) into v_current_user_qty
 152 |   from public.item_claims
 153 |   where item_id = p_item_id and user_id = p_user_id;
 154 | 
 155 |   select coalesce(sum(quantity), 0) into v_other_users_qty
 156 |   from public.item_claims
 157 |   where item_id = p_item_id and user_id <> p_user_id;
 158 | 
 159 |   v_prev_total := v_current_user_qty + v_other_users_qty;
 160 |   v_next_user_qty := greatest(0, v_current_user_qty + p_delta);
 161 |   v_next_total := v_other_users_qty + v_next_user_qty;
 162 | 
 163 |   -- Enforce max_cases cap if set on a case_split item
 164 |   if v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_max_cases is not null and v_max_cases > 0 then
 165 |     v_max_allowed_units := v_max_cases * v_case_size;
 166 |     if v_next_total > v_max_allowed_units then
 167 |       v_next_user_qty := greatest(0, v_max_allowed_units - v_other_users_qty);
 168 |       v_next_total := v_other_users_qty + v_next_user_qty;
 169 |       v_capped := true;
 170 |     end if;
 171 |   end if;
 172 | 
 173 |   -- Persist updated claim
 174 |   if v_next_user_qty <= 0 then
 175 |     delete from public.item_claims
 176 |     where item_id = p_item_id and user_id = p_user_id;
 177 |   else
 178 |     insert into public.item_claims (pool_id, item_id, user_id, user_name, avatar_color, quantity, updated_at)
 179 |     values (p_pool_id, p_item_id, p_user_id, p_user_name, p_avatar_color, v_next_user_qty, now())
 180 |     on conflict (item_id, user_id) do update
 181 |     set quantity = excluded.quantity,
 182 |         user_name = excluded.user_name,
 183 |         avatar_color = excluded.avatar_color,
 184 |         updated_at = now();
 185 |   end if;
 186 | 
 187 |   if v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_case_size > 1 then
 188 |     v_prev_full_cases := floor(v_prev_total::numeric / v_case_size);
 189 |     v_next_full_cases := floor(v_next_total::numeric / v_case_size);
 190 |     v_remainder := mod(v_next_total, v_case_size);
 191 |   else
 192 |     v_prev_full_cases := v_prev_total;
 193 |     v_next_full_cases := v_next_total;
 194 |     v_remainder := 0;
 195 |   end if;
 196 | 
 197 |   return jsonb_build_object(
 198 |     'newUserQuantity', v_next_user_qty,
 199 |     'totalClaimedUnits', v_next_total,
 200 |     'fullCasesCount', v_next_full_cases,
 201 |     'remainderUnits', v_remainder,
 202 |     'justCompletedCase', (v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_next_full_cases > v_prev_full_cases and v_remainder = 0),
 203 |     'justOpenedNewCase', (v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_prev_total > 0 and mod(v_prev_total, v_case_size) = 0 and v_next_total > v_prev_total),
 204 |     'cappedAtMaxCases', v_capped
 205 |   );
 206 | end;
 207 | $$;
 208 | 
 209 | -- 7. Enable Row Level Security (Open for MVP prototyping so neighbors can read/write)
 210 | alter table public.pools enable row level security;
 211 | alter table public.pool_participants enable row level security;
 212 | alter table public.catalog_items enable row level security;
 213 | alter table public.item_claims enable row level security;
 214 | alter table public.pool_messages enable row level security;
 215 | 
 216 | drop policy if exists "Allow public read/write on pools" on public.pools;
 217 | create policy "Allow public read/write on pools" on public.pools for all using (true) with check (true);
 218 | 
 219 | drop policy if exists "Allow public read/write on pool_participants" on public.pool_participants;
 220 | create policy "Allow public read/write on pool_participants" on public.pool_participants for all using (true) with check (true);
 221 | 
 222 | drop policy if exists "Allow public read/write on catalog_items" on public.catalog_items;
 223 | create policy "Allow public read/write on catalog_items" on public.catalog_items for all using (true) with check (true);
 224 | 
 225 | drop policy if exists "Allow public read/write on item_claims" on public.item_claims;
 226 | create policy "Allow public read/write on item_claims" on public.item_claims for all using (true) with check (true);
 227 | 
 228 | drop policy if exists "Allow public read/write on pool_messages" on public.pool_messages;
 229 | create policy "Allow public read/write on pool_messages" on public.pool_messages for all using (true) with check (true);
 230 | 
 231 | -- 8. Enable Supabase Realtime on all 5 tables
 232 | do $$
 233 | begin
 234 |   if not exists (
 235 |     select 1 from pg_publication_tables
 236 |     where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and tablename = 'pools'
 237 |   ) then
 238 |     alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.pools;
 239 |   end if;
 240 | 
 241 |   if not exists (
 242 |     select 1 from pg_publication_tables
 243 |     where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and tablename = 'pool_participants'
 244 |   ) then
 245 |     alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.pool_participants;
 246 |   end if;
 247 | 
 248 |   if not exists (
 249 |     select 1 from pg_publication_tables
 250 |     where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and tablename = 'catalog_items'
 251 |   ) then
 252 |     alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.catalog_items;
 253 |   end if;
 254 | 
 255 |   if not exists (
 256 |     select 1 from pg_publication_tables
 257 |     where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and tablename = 'item_claims'
 258 |   ) then
 259 |     alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.item_claims;
 260 |   end if;
 261 | 
 262 |   if not exists (
 263 |     select 1 from pg_publication_tables
 264 |     where pubname = 'supabase_realtime' and tablename = 'pool_messages'
 265 |   ) then
 266 |     alter publication supabase_realtime add table public.pool_messages;
 267 |   end if;
 268 | end $$;

+/**
+ * Local in-memory / AsyncStorage equivalent of the Postgres `claim_item_share` RPC:
+ * Enforces `maxCases` cap, computes whether a case just locked (`justCompletedCase`)
+ * or rolled over into a new case (`justOpenedNewCase`).
+ */
+export function evaluateAtomicClaimTransition(
+  item: CatalogItem,
+  user: Participant,
+  delta: number
+): {
+  updatedClaims: ParticipantShare[];
+  result: AtomicClaimResult;
+} {
+  const existingUserClaim = item.claims.find((c) => c.userId === user.id);
+  const currentUserQty = existingUserClaim ? existingUserClaim.quantity : 0;
+  const otherUsersQty = item.claims
+    .filter((c) => c.userId !== user.id)
+    .reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.quantity, 0);
+
+  const prevTotal = currentUserQty + otherUsersQty;
+  let nextUserQty = Math.max(0, currentUserQty + delta);
+  let nextTotal = otherUsersQty + nextUserQty;
+  let cappedAtMaxCases = false;
+
+  const caseSize = item.mode === 'case_split' && item.caseSize ? item.caseSize : 1;
+
+  if (
+    item.mode === 'case_split' &&
+    item.maxCases &&
+    item.maxCases > 0
+  ) {
+    const maxAllowedUnits = item.maxCases * caseSize;
+    if (nextTotal > maxAllowedUnits) {
+      nextUserQty = Math.max(0, maxAllowedUnits - otherUsersQty);
+      nextTotal = otherUsersQty + nextUserQty;
+      cappedAtMaxCases = true;
+    }
+  }
+
+  let updatedClaims = [...item.claims];
+  if (nextUserQty <= 0) {
+    updatedClaims = updatedClaims.filter((c) => c.userId !== user.id);
+  } else if (existingUserClaim) {
+    updatedClaims = updatedClaims.map((c) =>
+      c.userId === user.id ? { ...c, quantity: nextUserQty } : c
+    );
+  } else {
+    updatedClaims.push({
+      userId: user.id,
+      userName: user.name,
+      avatarColor: user.avatarColor,
+      quantity: nextUserQty,
+    });
+  }
+
+  const prevFullCases =
+    item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1
+      ? Math.floor(prevTotal / caseSize)
+      : prevTotal;
+  const nextFullCases =
+    item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1
+      ? Math.floor(nextTotal / caseSize)
+      : nextTotal;
+  const remainderUnits =
+    item.mode === 'case_split' && caseSize > 1 ? nextTotal % caseSize : 0;
+
+  const justCompletedCase =
+    item.mode === 'case_split' &&
+    caseSize > 1 &&
+    nextFullCases > prevFullCases &&
+    remainderUnits === 0;
+
+  const justOpenedNewCase =
+    item.mode === 'case_split' &&
+    caseSize > 1 &&
+    prevTotal > 0 &&
+    prevTotal % caseSize === 0 &&
+    nextTotal > prevTotal;
+
+  return {
+    updatedClaims,
+    result: {
+      newUserQuantity: nextUserQty,
+      totalClaimedUnits: nextTotal,
+      fullCasesCount: nextFullCases,
+      remainderUnits,
+      justCompletedCase,
+      justOpenedNewCase,
+      cappedAtMaxCases,
+    },
+  };
+}
+

@@ -381,6 +472,43 @@ export const PoolRepository = {

+  /**
+   * Calls the PostgreSQL `claim_item_share` RPC (`FOR UPDATE` row-level lock) when
+   * connected to Supabase, falling back to direct table upsert if the user hasn't run
+   * the updated SQL function yet.
+   */
+  async syncAtomicClaimDelta(
+    poolId: string,
+    itemId: string,
+    user: Participant,
+    delta: number,
+    fallbackQuantity: number
+  ): Promise<{ rpcResult?: AtomicClaimResult; error?: string }> {
+    const client = getOrCreateSupabaseClient();
+    if (!client) return {};
+
+    try {
+      const { data, error } = await client.rpc('claim_item_share', {
+        p_pool_id: poolId,
+        p_item_id: itemId,
+        p_user_id: user.id,
+        p_user_name: user.name,
+        p_avatar_color: user.avatarColor,
+        p_neighborhood: user.neighborhood,
+        p_delta: delta,
+      });
+
+      if (!error && data) {
+        return { rpcResult: data as AtomicClaimResult };
+      }
+
+      // Fallback to standard upsert if RPC isn't created yet
+      return await this.syncClaimQuantity(poolId, itemId, user, fallbackQuantity);
+    } catch (err: any) {
+      return { error: err?.message || 'Atomic claim RPC failed' };
+    }
+  },

--- 6. Enable Row Level Security (Open for MVP prototyping so neighbors can read/write)
+-- 6. Atomic Case-Split Claim RPC Function (`claim_item_share`)
+-- Uses `FOR UPDATE` row-level locking on `catalog_items` and `pools` so if two
+-- neighbors tap the last share of a bulk case at the exact same millisecond:
+--   • It serializes their claims cleanly without lost updates
+--   • Rejects mutations if the pool was locked by the host
+--   • Enforces `max_cases` cap if the host capped the number of bulk cases
+--   • Returns exact case completion & rollover metadata to trigger notifications
+create or replace function public.claim_item_share(
+  p_pool_id text,
+  p_item_id text,
+  p_user_id text,
+  p_user_name text,
+  p_avatar_color text,
+  p_neighborhood text,
+  p_delta integer
+)
+returns jsonb
+language plpgsql
+security definer
+as $$
+declare
+  v_pool_status text;
+  v_item_mode text;
+  v_case_size integer;
+  v_max_cases integer;
+  v_current_user_qty integer := 0;
+  v_other_users_qty integer := 0;
+  v_prev_total integer := 0;
+  v_next_user_qty integer := 0;
+  v_next_total integer := 0;
+  v_max_allowed_units integer;
+  v_capped boolean := false;
+  v_prev_full_cases integer := 0;
+  v_next_full_cases integer := 0;
+  v_remainder integer := 0;
+begin
+  -- Lock pool row to verify cutoff lock status
+  select status into v_pool_status
+  from public.pools
+  where id = p_pool_id
+  for update;
+
+  if v_pool_status = 'locked' then
+    raise exception 'Pool is locked for wholesale ordering; claims cannot be modified.';
+  end if;
+
+  -- Lock catalog_item row to serialize concurrent case-split claims
+  select mode, coalesce(case_size, 1), max_cases
+  into v_item_mode, v_case_size, v_max_cases
+  from public.catalog_items
+  where id = p_item_id and pool_id = p_pool_id
+  for update;
+
+  if not found then
+    raise exception 'Catalog item not found in pool.';
+  end if;
+
+  -- Ensure participant exists in pool_participants
+  insert into public.pool_participants (pool_id, user_id, user_name, neighborhood, avatar_color)
+  values (p_pool_id, p_user_id, p_user_name, coalesce(p_neighborhood, 'Victoria Gardens'), p_avatar_color)
+  on conflict (pool_id, user_id) do update
+  set user_name = excluded.user_name,
+      avatar_color = excluded.avatar_color,
+      updated_at = now();
+
+  -- Compute current quantities under lock
+  select coalesce(quantity, 0) into v_current_user_qty
+  from public.item_claims
+  where item_id = p_item_id and user_id = p_user_id;
+
+  select coalesce(sum(quantity), 0) into v_other_users_qty
+  from public.item_claims
+  where item_id = p_item_id and user_id <> p_user_id;
+
+  v_prev_total := v_current_user_qty + v_other_users_qty;
+  v_next_user_qty := greatest(0, v_current_user_qty + p_delta);
+  v_next_total := v_other_users_qty + v_next_user_qty;
+
+  -- Enforce max_cases cap if set on a case_split item
+  if v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_max_cases is not null and v_max_cases > 0 then
+    v_max_allowed_units := v_max_cases * v_case_size;
+    if v_next_total > v_max_allowed_units then
+      v_next_user_qty := greatest(0, v_max_allowed_units - v_other_users_qty);
+      v_next_total := v_other_users_qty + v_next_user_qty;
+      v_capped := true;
+    end if;
+  end if;
+
+  -- Persist updated claim
+  if v_next_user_qty <= 0 then
+    delete from public.item_claims
+    where item_id = p_item_id and user_id = p_user_id;
+  else
+    insert into public.item_claims (pool_id, item_id, user_id, user_name, avatar_color, quantity, updated_at)
+    values (p_pool_id, p_item_id, p_user_id, p_user_name, p_avatar_color, v_next_user_qty, now())
+    on conflict (item_id, user_id) do update
+    set quantity = excluded.quantity,
+        user_name = excluded.user_name,
+        avatar_color = excluded.avatar_color,
+        updated_at = now();
+  end if;
+
+  if v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_case_size > 1 then
+    v_prev_full_cases := floor(v_prev_total::numeric / v_case_size);
+    v_next_full_cases := floor(v_next_total::numeric / v_case_size);
+    v_remainder := mod(v_next_total, v_case_size);
+  else
+    v_prev_full_cases := v_prev_total;
+    v_next_full_cases := v_next_total;
+    v_remainder := 0;
+  end if;
+
+  return jsonb_build_object(
+    'newUserQuantity', v_next_user_qty,
+    'totalClaimedUnits', v_next_total,
+    'fullCasesCount', v_next_full_cases,
+    'remainderUnits', v_remainder,
+    'justCompletedCase', (v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_next_full_cases > v_prev_full_cases and v_remainder = 0),
+    'justOpenedNewCase', (v_item_mode = 'case_split' and v_prev_total > 0 and mod(v_prev_total, v_case_size) = 0 and v_next_total > v_prev_total),
+    'cappedAtMaxCases', v_capped
+  );
+end;
+$$;
+
